/**
 * AttendSure Web API Client - Strict /api/v1/ Enforcement, SWR Cache & JWT Layer
 * File: web/src/api/client.ts
 */

import axios, {
  AxiosError,
  CanceledError,
  type AxiosRequestConfig,
  type AxiosResponse,
  type InternalAxiosRequestConfig,
} from 'axios';
import { API_ROUTES } from './routes';

const FRESH_TTL_MS = 25_000;
const STALE_TTL_MS = 300_000;
const MAX_CACHE_ENTRIES = 150;

interface CacheEntry {
  freshUntil: number;
  staleUntil: number;
  response: AxiosResponse;
}

type CacheListener = (data: unknown) => void;

const responseCache = new Map<string, CacheEntry>();
const inflightGetMap = new Map<string, Promise<AxiosResponse>>();
const cacheListeners = new Map<string, Set<CacheListener>>();

// Safety map ensuring any residual unversioned path resolves to its canonical /v1/ endpoint
const LEGACY_TO_V1_PREFIXES: ReadonlyArray<[RegExp, string]> = [
  [/^me\/?$/i, 'v1/accounts/me/'],
  [/^users(\/|$)/i, 'v1/accounts/users$1'],
  [/^facultys(\/|$)/i, 'v1/accounts/teachers$1'],
  [/^faculty\/dropdown\/?$/i, 'v1/accounts/teachers/dropdown/'],
  [/^faculty\/(\d+)\/reset-device\/?$/i, 'v1/accounts/teachers/$1/reset-device/'],
  [/^faculty(\/|$)/i, 'v1/accounts/teachers$1'],
  [/^activity-logs(\/|$)/i, 'v1/accounts/audit-logs$1'],
  [/^settings\/school\/?$/i, 'v1/school/settings/'],
  [/^academic-years(\/|$)/i, 'v1/school/school-years$1'],
  [/^grade-levels(\/|$)/i, 'v1/school/grade-levels$1'],
  [/^rooms(\/|$)/i, 'v1/school/rooms$1'],
  [/^subjects(\/|$)/i, 'v1/school/subjects$1'],
  [/^sections(\/|$)/i, 'v1/school/sections$1'],
  [/^schedules(\/|$)/i, 'v1/school/periods$1'],
  [/^students(\/|$)/i, 'v1/students$1'],
  [/^gate-passes(\/|$)/i, 'v1/attendance/exit-passes$1'],
  [/^classroom\/adviser-matrix\/?$/i, 'v1/attendance/adviser-matrix/'],
  [/^classroom\/(student_list|class_list)\/?$/i, 'v1/attendance/classes/'],
  [/^geofence\/audit\/?$/i, 'v1/campus/audit/'],
  [/^geofence(\/|$)/i, 'v1/campus/boundary$1'],
  [/^enterprise\/emergency-rollcall\/?$/i, 'v1/campus/roll-call/'],
  [/^enterprise\/sdg-impact\/?$/i, 'v1/reports/sdg-impact/'],
  [/^enterprise\/sardo-early-warning\/?$/i, 'v1/reports/dropout-risk/'],
  [/^dashboard\/overview\/?$/i, 'v1/reports/dashboard/'],
  [/^system\/status\/?$/i, 'v1/reports/system-status/'],
  [/^reports(\/|$)/i, 'v1/reports$1'],
  [/^notifications(\/|$)/i, 'v1/messaging/notifications$1'],
  [/^chat\/threads\/?$/i, 'v1/messaging/threads/'],
  [/^chat\/messages\/?$/i, 'v1/messaging/messages/'],
  [/^core\/maintenance\/db-check\/?$/i, 'v1/health/db/'],
  [/^core\/maintenance\/flush-cache\/?$/i, 'v1/cache/flush/'],
];

const notifyCacheListeners = (key: string, data: unknown): void => {
  const listeners = cacheListeners.get(key);
  if (!listeners) return;
  listeners.forEach((fn) => {
    try {
      fn(data);
    } catch {
      // Isolate listener errors
    }
  });
};

const pruneCacheIfNeeded = (): void => {
  if (responseCache.size <= MAX_CACHE_ENTRIES) return;
  const oldestKey = responseCache.keys().next().value;
  if (oldestKey) responseCache.delete(oldestKey);
};

export const clearApiClientCache = (): void => {
  responseCache.clear();
  inflightGetMap.clear();
};

export const invalidateApiCache = (): void => {
  const now = Date.now();
  responseCache.forEach((entry) => {
    entry.freshUntil = now - 1;
  });
};

const isLocalOrLanHost = (hostname: string): boolean =>
  hostname === 'localhost' ||
  hostname === '127.0.0.1' ||
  hostname.startsWith('192.168.') ||
  hostname.startsWith('10.') ||
  hostname.startsWith('172.') ||
  hostname.endsWith('.local');

export const resolveBaseUrl = (): string => {
  const envUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL;
  if (envUrl && typeof envUrl === 'string' && envUrl.trim()) {
    return envUrl.trim().replace(/\/+$/, '').replace(/\/api(\/v1)?$/i, '');
  }

  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    const { protocol, hostname, port } = window.location;
    if (port === '80' || port === '443' || port === '5173' || !port) {
      if (hostname.includes('attendsure.com.ph') || hostname.includes('trycloudflare.com')) {
        return `${protocol}//${hostname}`;
      }
    }
    if (isLocalOrLanHost(hostname)) {
      return `${protocol}//${hostname}:8000`;
    }
    return port && port !== '80' && port !== '443'
      ? `${protocol}//${hostname}:${port}`
      : `${protocol}//${hostname}`;
  }

  return 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';
};

export const normalizeApiPath = (rawUrl: string): string => {
  const trimmed = rawUrl.trim();
  if (/^(javascript|vbscript|data):/i.test(trimmed)) {
    throw new Error('Blocked unsafe URL protocol.');
  }
  if (trimmed.startsWith('http://') || trimmed.startsWith('https://')) {
    return trimmed;
  }

  let clean = trimmed
    .replace(/^\/+/, '')
    .replace(/\.\.\//g, '')
    .replace(/^(api\/)+/i, '');

  if (!clean.startsWith('v1/')) {
    const qIdx = clean.indexOf('?');
    const pathPart = qIdx >= 0 ? clean.slice(0, qIdx) : clean;
    const queryPart = qIdx >= 0 ? clean.slice(qIdx) : '';

    let mapped = pathPart;
    for (const [pattern, replacement] of LEGACY_TO_V1_PREFIXES) {
      if (pattern.test(pathPart)) {
        mapped = pathPart.replace(pattern, replacement);
        break;
      }
    }
    clean = (mapped.startsWith('v1/') ? mapped : `v1/${mapped}`) + queryPart;
  }

  return `/api/${clean}`;
};

const serializeParams = (params?: Record<string, unknown>): string => {
  if (!params || typeof params !== 'object') return '';
  const entries = Object.entries(params)
    .filter(([k, v]) => v !== undefined && v !== '' && k !== 'nocache' && k !== '_t' && k !== 'silent')
    .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
  return entries.length > 0 ? JSON.stringify(entries) : '';
};

export const buildApiCacheKey = (url: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>): string => {
  const normUrl = normalizeApiPath(url);
  const userScope =
    localStorage.getItem('attendsure_user_id') ||
    localStorage.getItem('attendsure_username') ||
    'guest';
  return `${userScope}:${normUrl}?${serializeParams(params)}`;
};

export function peekApiCache<T = unknown>(url: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>): T | undefined {
  const key = buildApiCacheKey(url, params);
  const hit = responseCache.get(key);
  if (!hit || hit.staleUntil <= Date.now()) return undefined;
  return hit.response.data as T;
}

export const subscribeApiCache = (
  url: string,
  params: Record<string, unknown> | undefined,
  listener: CacheListener
): (() => void) => {
  const key = buildApiCacheKey(url, params);
  let set = cacheListeners.get(key);
  if (!set) {
    set = new Set();
    cacheListeners.set(key, set);
  }
  set.add(listener);
  return () => {
    const current = cacheListeners.get(key);
    if (!current) return;
    current.delete(listener);
    if (current.size === 0) cacheListeners.delete(key);
  };
};

const clearStoredAuth = (): void => {
  const keys = [
    'attendsure_access',
    'attendsure_refresh',
    'attendsure_token',
    'auth_token',
    'token',
    'attendsure_username',
    'attendsure_faculty_name',
    'attendsure_role',
    'attendsure_user_id',
    'attendsure_user_photo',
    'attendsure_can_teach',
  ];
  keys.forEach((k) => localStorage.removeItem(k));
  clearApiClientCache();
};

const apiClient = axios.create({
  timeout: Number(import.meta.env.VITE_API_TIMEOUT_MS) || 10000,
  headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
});

apiClient.interceptors.request.use(
  (config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {
    config.baseURL = resolveBaseUrl();
    config.url = normalizeApiPath(config.url || '');

    const method = (config.method || 'get').toLowerCase();
    if (method !== 'get') {
      invalidateApiCache();
    }

    if (config.headers && !config.headers.Authorization) {
      const jwtAccess = localStorage.getItem('attendsure_access')?.trim().replace(/[\r\n]/g, '');
      const legacyToken = (
        localStorage.getItem('attendsure_token') ||
        localStorage.getItem('auth_token') ||
        localStorage.getItem('token') ||
        ''
      )
        .trim()
        .replace(/[\r\n]/g, '');

      if (jwtAccess) {
        config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${jwtAccess}`;
      } else if (legacyToken) {
        config.headers.Authorization = `Token ${legacyToken}`;
      }
    }

    return config;
  },
  (error) => Promise.reject(error)
);

let refreshPromise: Promise<string | null> | null = null;

apiClient.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  async (error: AxiosError) => {
    const originalRequest = error.config as (InternalAxiosRequestConfig & { _retried?: boolean }) | undefined;
    const reqUrl = originalRequest?.url || '';
    const isAuthRoute = reqUrl.includes('/login/') || reqUrl.includes('/token/');

    if (error.response?.status === 401 && originalRequest && !originalRequest._retried && !isAuthRoute) {
      const refreshToken = localStorage.getItem('attendsure_refresh')?.trim();
      if (refreshToken) {
        originalRequest._retried = true;
        if (!refreshPromise) {
          refreshPromise = axios
            .post(`${resolveBaseUrl()}/api/v1/accounts/token/refresh/`, { refresh: refreshToken }, { timeout: 6000 })
            .then((res) => {
              const nextAccess = res.data?.access as string | undefined;
              const nextRefresh = res.data?.refresh as string | undefined;
              if (nextAccess) {
                localStorage.setItem('attendsure_access', nextAccess);
                if (nextRefresh) localStorage.setItem('attendsure_refresh', nextRefresh);
                return nextAccess;
              }
              return null;
            })
            .catch(() => null)
            .finally(() => {
              refreshPromise = null;
            });
        }

        const newAccess = await refreshPromise;
        if (newAccess) {
          originalRequest.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${newAccess}`;
          return apiClient(originalRequest);
        }
      }

      const fallbackToken = localStorage.getItem('attendsure_token')?.trim();
      if (fallbackToken && String(originalRequest.headers?.Authorization || '').startsWith('Bearer ')) {
        originalRequest._retried = true;
        localStorage.removeItem('attendsure_access');
        originalRequest.headers.Authorization = `Token ${fallbackToken}`;
        return apiClient(originalRequest);
      }

      clearStoredAuth();
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('attendsure:auth-expired'));
      }
    }

    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

interface GenericAbortSignal {
  readonly aborted: boolean;
  addEventListener?: (type: string, listener: () => void, options?: { once?: boolean }) => void;
  removeEventListener?: (type: string, listener: () => void) => void;
}

const withCallerAbort = <R>(promise: Promise<R>, signal?: GenericAbortSignal): Promise<R> => {
  if (!signal) return promise;
  if (signal.aborted) {
    return Promise.reject(new CanceledError('canceled'));
  }
  return new Promise<R>((resolve, reject) => {
    const onAbort = () => reject(new CanceledError('canceled'));
    signal.addEventListener?.('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
    promise.then(
      (val) => {
        signal.removeEventListener?.('abort', onAbort);
        resolve(val);
      },
      (err) => {
        signal.removeEventListener?.('abort', onAbort);
        reject(err);
      }
    );
  });
};

const rawGet = apiClient.get.bind(apiClient);

const fetchAndPopulateCache = <T = unknown, D = unknown>(
  url: string,
  key: string,
  config?: AxiosRequestConfig<D>
): Promise<AxiosResponse<T>> => {
  const existing = inflightGetMap.get(key);
  if (existing) return existing as Promise<AxiosResponse<T>>;

  const sharedConfig: AxiosRequestConfig<D> = { ...(config || {}), signal: undefined };
  const req = rawGet<T, AxiosResponse<T>, D>(url, sharedConfig)
    .then((res) => {
      const now = Date.now();
      pruneCacheIfNeeded();
      responseCache.set(key, {
        freshUntil: now + FRESH_TTL_MS,
        staleUntil: now + STALE_TTL_MS,
        response: res,
      });
      notifyCacheListeners(key, res.data);
      return res;
    })
    .finally(() => {
      inflightGetMap.delete(key);
    });

  inflightGetMap.set(key, req);
  return req;
};

apiClient.get = function swrGet<T = any, R = AxiosResponse<T>, D = any>(
  url: string,
  config?: AxiosRequestConfig<D>
): Promise<R> {
  const paramsObj = (config?.params || undefined) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  const forceNetwork = Boolean(paramsObj?._t || paramsObj?.nocache);
  const key = buildApiCacheKey(url, paramsObj);
  const now = Date.now();

  if (!forceNetwork) {
    const hit = responseCache.get(key);
    if (hit && hit.freshUntil > now) {
      return withCallerAbort(Promise.resolve(hit.response as unknown as R), config?.signal as GenericAbortSignal);
    }
    if (hit && hit.staleUntil > now) {
      void fetchAndPopulateCache<T, D>(url, key, config).catch(() => {});
      return withCallerAbort(Promise.resolve(hit.response as unknown as R), config?.signal as GenericAbortSignal);
    }
  }

  return withCallerAbort(
    fetchAndPopulateCache<T, D>(url, key, config) as unknown as Promise<R>,
    config?.signal as GenericAbortSignal
  );
};

export const prefetchApiGet = (url: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>): void => {
  const hasToken = Boolean(localStorage.getItem('attendsure_access') || localStorage.getItem('attendsure_token'));
  if (!hasToken) return;
  const key = buildApiCacheKey(url, params);
  const hit = responseCache.get(key);
  if (hit && hit.freshUntil > Date.now()) return;
  void fetchAndPopulateCache(url, key, { params }).catch(() => {});
};

let lastWarmupUser = '';

/** Gently preloads only 3 lightweight (<1KB) lookup endpoints once per user login. */
export const warmupRoleEndpoints = (role: string, canTeach: boolean): void => {
  const userScope = localStorage.getItem('attendsure_username') || '';
  if (!userScope || lastWarmupUser === userScope) return;
  lastWarmupUser = userScope;

  const upper = role.toUpperCase();
  const isLeader = ['ADMIN', 'SUPERADMIN', 'PRINCIPAL', 'DEPT_HEAD'].includes(upper);

  const queue: Array<[string, Record<string, unknown>?]> = [
    [API_ROUTES.students.metrics, undefined],
    [API_ROUTES.school.sections, { page_size: 300 }],
  ];
  if (canTeach || isLeader) {
    queue.push([API_ROUTES.attendance.classes, { summary: 1 }]);
  }

  queue.forEach(([endpoint, params], index) => {
    setTimeout(() => prefetchApiGet(endpoint, params), 400 + index * 450);
  });
};

export default apiClient;

export const BASE_ROLE_PERMISSIONS: Record<string, NavItemKey[]> = {
  ADMIN: [
    'dashboard', 'classroom-scanner', 'my-classes', 'advisory-monitor',
    'students', 'facultys', 'users', 'gate-passes', 'schedules',
    'dtr', 'geofence', 'messages', 'push', 'reports', 'settings',

  ],
};

/** Preloads both the Vite code chunk and PostgreSQL JSON data when hovering a Sidebar item. */
let hoverPrefetchTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

const prefetchTabResources = (tab: NavItemKey): void => {
  if (hoverPrefetchTimer) clearTimeout(hoverPrefetchTimer);
  hoverPrefetchTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    switch (tab) {
      case 'dashboard':
        void loadDashboard();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.reports.dashboard);
        break;
      case 'classroom-scanner':
        void loadScanner();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.attendance.classes, { summary: 1 });
        break;
      case 'my-classes':
        void loadMyClasses();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.attendance.classes);
        break;
      case 'advisory-monitor':
        void loadAdvisory();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.attendance.adviserMatrix);
        break;
      case 'students':
        void loadStudents();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.students.metrics);
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.students.list, { page: 1, page_size: 25 });
        break;
      case 'facultys':
        void loadFaculty();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.accounts.teachers, { page: 1, page_size: 25 });
        break;
      case 'users':
        void loadUsers();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.accounts.users, { page_size: 'ALL' });
        break;
      case 'gate-passes':
        void loadGatePasses();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.attendance.exitPasses);
        break;
      case 'schedules':
        void loadSchedules();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.school.periods, { page_size: 500 });
        break;
      case 'dtr':
        void loadDtr();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.reports.dtr, { month: now.getMonth() + 1, year: now.getFullYear() });
        break;
      case 'geofence':
        void loadGeofence();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.campus.boundary);
        break;
      case 'messages':
        void loadParentChat();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.messaging.threads);
        break;
      case 'push':
        void loadPush();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.messaging.notifications, { page_size: 100 });
        break;
      case 'reports':
        void loadSf1();
        void loadSf2();
        void loadSf4();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.school.sections);
        break;
      case 'settings':
        void loadSettings();
        prefetchApiGet(API_ROUTES.school.settings);
        break;
      default:
        break;
    }
  }, 120);
};

const SubtleSkeletonFallback: React.FC = () => {
  const { tokens } = useTheme();
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 20, display: 'grid', gap: 12 }}>
      <div style={{ height: 48, borderRadius: 12, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurface, opacity: 0.65 }} />
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr))', gap: 12 }}>
        {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((n) => (
          <div key={n} style={{ height: 84, borderRadius: 12, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurface, opacity: 0.65 }} />
        ))}
      </div>
      <div style={{ height: 280, borderRadius: 14, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurface, opacity: 0.65 }} />
    </div>
  );
};

const AppContent: React.FC = () => {
  const { school, loading: isSchoolLoading } = useSchool();
  const { tokens, brand } = useTheme();

  const [session, setSession] = useState<UserSession | null>(() => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('attendsure_token') || localStorage.getItem('attendsure_access');
    if (!token) return null;
    const username = localStorage.getItem('attendsure_username') || '';
    return {
      user_id: Number(localStorage.getItem('attendsure_user_id') || 0),
      token,
      username,
      faculty_name: localStorage.getItem('attendsure_faculty_name') || username || 'Staff User',
      role: localStorage.getItem('attendsure_role') || 'FACULTY',
    };
  });

  const [userPhoto, setUserPhoto] = useState<string | null>(() => localStorage.getItem('attendsure_user_photo'));
  const [canTeach, setCanTeach] = useState<boolean>(() => localStorage.getItem('attendsure_can_teach') === 'true');
  const [isCompleteProfileModalOpen, setIsCompleteProfileModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isProfileModalOpen, setIsProfileModalOpen] = useState(false);

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<NavItemKey>('dashboard');
  const [selectedReport, setSelectedReport] = useState<'sf1' | 'sf2' | 'sf4'>('sf1');
  const [isSidebarCollapsed, setIsSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [isServerOnline, setIsServerOnline] = useState(true);
  const [isCheckingServer, setIsCheckingServer] = useState(false);

  const userRole = (session?.role || '').toUpperCase();

  const allowedTabs = useMemo<NavItemKey[]>(() => {
    if (!userRole) return [];
    const tabs = [...(BASE_ROLE_PERMISSIONS[userRole] || BASE_ROLE_PERMISSIONS.FACULTY)];
    if (canTeach) {
      (['my-classes', 'classroom-scanner', 'advisory-monitor', 'messages'] as NavItemKey[]).forEach((t) => {
        if (!tabs.includes(t)) tabs.push(t);
      });
    }
    return tabs;
  }, [userRole, canTeach]);

  const isAllowed = allowedTabs.includes(activeTab);

  // Warm up primary endpoints & code chunks right after sign-in without blocking UI paint
useEffect(() => {
    if (!session?.token || !userRole) return;
    warmupRoleEndpoints(userRole, canTeach);
    const chunkTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      void loadMyClasses();
      void loadScanner();
      void loadStudents();
      void loadDtr();
    }, 800);
    return () => clearTimeout(chunkTimer);
  }, [session?.token, userRole, canTeach]);

  const handleAuthExpired = useCallback(() => {
    localStorage.removeItem('attendsure_access');
    localStorage.removeItem('attendsure_refresh');
    localStorage.removeItem('attendsure_token');
    localStorage.removeItem('attendsure_username');
    localStorage.removeItem('attendsure_faculty_name');
    localStorage.removeItem('attendsure_role');
    localStorage.removeItem('attendsure_user_id');
    localStorage.removeItem('attendsure_user_photo');
    localStorage.removeItem('attendsure_can_teach');
    setSession(null);
    setUserPhoto(null);
    setCanTeach(false);
    setIsCompleteProfileModalOpen(false);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    window.addEventListener('attendsure:auth-expired', handleAuthExpired);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('attendsure:auth-expired', handleAuthExpired);
  }, [handleAuthExpired]);

  const handleTabSelection = useCallback(
    (tab: NavItemKey) => {
      if (!allowedTabs.includes(tab)) return;
      startTransition(() => {
        setActiveTab(tab);
      });
    },
    [allowedTabs]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const onNavigate = (e: Event) => {
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent<string | { tab: string; report?: string }>).detail;
      if (!detail) return;
      if (typeof detail === 'string') {
        handleTabSelection(detail as NavItemKey);
      } else if (detail.tab) {
        handleTabSelection(detail.tab as NavItemKey);
        if (detail.report && ['sf1', 'sf2', 'sf4'].includes(detail.report)) {
          startTransition(() => {
            setSelectedReport(detail.report as 'sf1' | 'sf2' | 'sf4');
          });
        }
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('attendsure:navigate', onNavigate);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('attendsure:navigate', onNavigate);
  }, [handleTabSelection]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session?.token) return;
    let active = true;
    apiClient
      .get(API_ROUTES.accounts.me)
      .then((res) => {
        if (!active || !res.data) return;
        setIsServerOnline(true);
        const d = res.data;
        const photo = d.photo_url || d.photo || null;
        const name = String(d.full_name || d.username || '').trim();
        const role = String(d.role || d.system_role || session.role || 'FACULTY').toUpperCase();
        const userId = Number(d.id || d.user_id || 0);
        const teachFlag = Boolean(d.can_teach || d.is_teaching || d.assigned_class_count > 0 || role === 'FACULTY');

        setUserPhoto(photo);
        if (photo) localStorage.setItem('attendsure_user_photo', photo);
        else localStorage.removeItem('attendsure_user_photo');

        if (name) localStorage.setItem('attendsure_faculty_name', name);
        localStorage.setItem('attendsure_role', role);
        if (userId) localStorage.setItem('attendsure_user_id', String(userId));
        setCanTeach(teachFlag);
        localStorage.setItem('attendsure_can_teach', String(teachFlag));

        if (d.is_profile_completed === false) setIsCompleteProfileModalOpen(true);

        setSession((prev) =>
          prev ? { ...prev, user_id: userId || prev.user_id, faculty_name: name || prev.faculty_name, role } : null
        );
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!active) return;
        if (!err.response) setIsServerOnline(false);
        else if (err.response?.status === 401) handleAuthExpired();
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [session?.token, handleAuthExpired]);

  const retryConnection = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsCheckingServer(true);
    try {
      await apiClient.get(API_ROUTES.health.db, { params: { nocache: 1 } });
      setIsServerOnline(true);
    } catch {
      setIsServerOnline(false);
    } finally {
      setIsCheckingServer(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const pageTitle = useMemo((): string => {
    if (!isAllowed) return 'Access Restricted';
    const prefix = school.school_name ? `${school.school_name} • ` : '';
    switch (activeTab) {
      case 'dashboard': return `${prefix}Dashboard`;
      case 'classroom-scanner': return 'Classroom QR Attendance Scanner';
      case 'my-classes': return 'My Classes & Student Lists';
      case 'advisory-monitor': return 'Advisory Attendance & Cutting Matrix';
      case 'students': return 'Student Directory & QR Cards';
      case 'facultys': return 'Teachers & Staff Directory';
      case 'users': return 'User Accounts & Roles';
      case 'gate-passes': return 'Campus Exit Passes';
      case 'schedules': return 'Sections & Class Schedules';
      case 'dtr': return 'Monthly Time Records (CSC Form 48)';
      case 'geofence': return 'Campus GPS Boundary';
      case 'messages': return 'Parent & Teacher Direct Chat';
      case 'push': return 'Mobile Push Alerts';
      case 'reports':
        return selectedReport === 'sf1'
          ? 'DepEd School Form 1 (Register)'
          : selectedReport === 'sf2'
          ? 'DepEd School Form 2 (Daily Attendance)'
          : 'DepEd School Form 4 (Monthly Movement)';
      case 'settings': return 'School Settings & Audit Logs';
      default: return 'AttendSure Portal';
    }
  }, [activeTab, isAllowed, school.school_name, selectedReport]);

  const renderTab = () => {
    if (!isAllowed) {
      return (
        <div style={{ padding: 48, textAlign: 'center' }}>
          <ShieldAlert size={36} color={brand.danger} style={{ margin: '0 auto 12px', display: 'block' }} />
          <h2 style={{ margin: '0 0 6px', color: tokens.textPrimary }}>Access Restricted</h2>
          <p style={{ color: tokens.textMuted, fontSize: '0.84rem' }}>Your role ({userRole}) cannot view this module.</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleTabSelection(allowedTabs[0] || 'dashboard')}
            style={{ marginTop: 14, padding: '8px 16px', backgroundColor: brand.buttonBg, color: '#fff', border: 'none', borderRadius: 8, fontWeight: 700, cursor: 'pointer', display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={14} /> Back to Dashboard
          </button>
        </div>
      );
    }

    switch (activeTab) {
      case 'dashboard':
        return (
          <DashboardView
            isServerOnline={isServerOnline}
            userRole={userRole}
            onManualRefresh={retryConnection}
            onNavigateTab={(tab, report) => {
              handleTabSelection(tab as NavItemKey);
              if (report && ['sf1', 'sf2', 'sf4'].includes(report)) {
                startTransition(() => setSelectedReport(report as 'sf1' | 'sf2' | 'sf4'));
              }
            }}
          />
        );
      case 'classroom-scanner':
        return <div style={{ padding: 'clamp(12px, 2vw, 20px)' }}><ClassroomScanner /></div>;
      case 'my-classes': return <MyAssignedClassesTab />;
      case 'advisory-monitor':
        return <div style={{ padding: 'clamp(12px, 2vw, 20px)' }}><AdvisorySubjectMonitor /></div>;
      case 'students': return <StudentsTab />;
      case 'facultys': return <FacultyTab />;
      case 'users': return <UsersTab />;
      case 'gate-passes': return <GatePassesTab />;
      case 'schedules': return <SchedulesTab />;
      case 'dtr': return <DTRTab />;
      case 'geofence': return <GeofenceTab />;
      case 'messages': return <ParentChatTab />;
      case 'push': return <PushNotificationsTab />;
      case 'reports':
        return (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%', width: '100%', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
            {selectedReport === 'sf1' && <SF1ReportTab activeReportId={selectedReport} onSelectReport={(id) => startTransition(() => setSelectedReport(id as 'sf1' | 'sf2' | 'sf4'))} />}
            {selectedReport === 'sf2' && <SF2ReportTab activeReportId={selectedReport} onSelectReport={(id) => startTransition(() => setSelectedReport(id as 'sf1' | 'sf2' | 'sf4'))} />}
            {selectedReport === 'sf4' && <SF4ReportTab activeReportId={selectedReport} onSelectReport={(id) => startTransition(() => setSelectedReport(id as 'sf1' | 'sf2' | 'sf4'))} />}
          </div>
        );
      case 'settings': return <SettingsTab />;
      default: return <DashboardView isServerOnline={isServerOnline} onManualRefresh={retryConnection} />;
    }
  };

  const resolvedSchoolLogo = school.school_logo || school.left_logo || null;

  return (
    <>
      <SplashScreen loading={isSchoolLoading} minDurationMs={0} />

      {!session ? (
        <LoginView
          onLoginSuccess={(nextSession) => {
            setSession(nextSession);
            setActiveTab('dashboard');
          }}
        />
      ) : (
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            height: '100vh',
            width: '100%',
            overflow: 'hidden',
            backgroundColor: tokens.bgApp,
            padding: 'clamp(6px, 1vw, 12px)',
            gap: 'clamp(6px, 1vw, 12px)',
            boxSizing: 'border-box',
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ height: '100%', borderRadius: 16, overflow: 'hidden', borderWidth: 1, borderStyle: 'solid', borderColor: tokens.borderSubtle, boxShadow: tokens.cardShadow, flexShrink: 0 }}>
            <Sidebar
              activeTab={activeTab}
              onSelectTab={handleTabSelection}
              onPrefetchTab={prefetchTabResources}
              session={session}
              userPhoto={userPhoto}
              isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed}
              onToggleCollapse={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed((p) => !p)}
              schoolName={school.school_name}
              schoolId={school.school_id}
              schoolLogo={resolvedSchoolLogo}
              canTeach={canTeach}
              allowedTabs={allowedTabs}
            />
          </div>

          <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, height: '100%', borderRadius: 16, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurface, borderWidth: 1, borderStyle: 'solid', borderColor: tokens.borderSubtle, boxShadow: tokens.cardShadow, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
            <Header
              title={pageTitle}
              isSystemOnline={isServerOnline}
              session={session}
              userPhoto={userPhoto}
              schoolName={school.school_name}
              schoolId={school.school_id}
              schoolLogo={resolvedSchoolLogo}
              onLogout={handleAuthExpired}
              onNavigateTab={handleTabSelection}
              onOpenProfile={() => setIsProfileModalOpen(true)}
            />

            <main style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, overflowY: activeTab === 'dashboard' || activeTab === 'reports' ? 'hidden' : 'auto', overflowX: 'hidden', position: 'relative', backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle }}>
              <ErrorBoundary featureName={pageTitle} resetKeys={[activeTab, selectedReport]}>
                <Suspense fallback={<SubtleSkeletonFallback />}>
                  {renderTab()}
                </Suspense>
              </ErrorBoundary>

              {!isServerOnline && (
                <div onClick={retryConnection} style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, zIndex: 35, backgroundColor: 'rgba(15,23,42,0.68)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                  <div style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: '10px 20px', borderRadius: 9999, backgroundColor: '#0f172a', color: '#f8fafc', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: '0.82rem' }}>
                    <WifiOff size={16} color="#38bdf8" />
                    {isCheckingServer ? 'Reconnecting...' : 'Server offline. Click to reconnect.'}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </main>

            <Footer
              schoolName={school.school_name}
              schoolId={school.school_id}
              divisionOrCity={school.division}
              isDatabaseConnected={isServerOnline}
              isGateSystemOnline={isServerOnline}
            />
          </div>

          <ProfileModal
            isOpen={isProfileModalOpen}
            onClose={() => setIsProfileModalOpen(false)}
            onProfileUpdated={(updated: ProfileUpdatePayload) => {
              const name = typeof updated.full_name === 'string' && updated.full_name.trim() ? updated.full_name.trim() : undefined;
              if (name) {
                localStorage.setItem('attendsure_faculty_name', name);
                setSession((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, faculty_name: name } : null));
              }
              if (updated.photo !== undefined) {
                setUserPhoto(updated.photo || null);
                if (updated.photo) localStorage.setItem('attendsure_user_photo', updated.photo);
                else localStorage.removeItem('attendsure_user_photo');
              }
            }}
          />

          <CompleteProfileModal
            isOpen={isCompleteProfileModalOpen}
            onCompleted={(updatedUser: CompletedProfileUser) => {
              setIsCompleteProfileModalOpen(false);
              const name = String(updatedUser.full_name || updatedUser.username || '').trim();
              const photo = (typeof updatedUser.photo_url === 'string' && updatedUser.photo_url) || (typeof updatedUser.photo === 'string' && updatedUser.photo) || null;
              if (name) {
                localStorage.setItem('attendsure_faculty_name', name);
                setSession((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, faculty_name: name } : null));
              }
              if (photo) {
                setUserPhoto(photo);
                localStorage.setItem('attendsure_user_photo', photo);
              }
            }}
          />
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
};

export const App: React.FC = () => (
  <ThemeProvider>
    <SchoolProvider>
      <AlertProvider>
        <AppContent />
      </AlertProvider>
    </SchoolProvider>
  </ThemeProvider>
);

export default App;

================================================================================
# FILE: web\src\components\common\PageSetupModal.tsx
================================================================================
/**
 * AttendSure - Universal Page Setup Modal
 * File: frontend/src/components/common/PageSetupModal.tsx
 *
 * Configures paper dimensions, margins, orientation, and layout format.
 * The official letterhead header is locked to Page 1 across all reports.
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  Check,
  RotateCw,
  FileText,
  Maximize2,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  Palette,
  BookmarkCheck,
  Undo2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { PAPER_SIZES, MARGIN_PRESETS } from '../../types/pageSetup';
import type {
  PageSetupConfig,
  PaperSizeKey,
  PageOrientation,
  LayoutMode,
  MarginPresetKey,
} from '../../types/pageSetup';

interface PageSetupModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  currentConfig: PageSetupConfig;
  onLiveChange?: (config: PageSetupConfig) => void;
  onApply: (config: PageSetupConfig, setAsDefault: boolean) => void;
  reportTitle?: string;
}

const PRESET_COLORS = [
  { label: 'Standard White', value: '#ffffff' },
  { label: 'Soft Ivory', value: '#fdfbf7' },
  { label: 'Clean Slate', value: '#f8fafc' },
  { label: 'Document Cream', value: '#fffdf5' },
];

export const PageSetupModal: React.FC<PageSetupModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  currentConfig,
  onLiveChange,
  onApply,
  reportTitle = 'Report',
}) => {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<PageSetupConfig>({
    ...currentConfig,
    headerRepeat: 'first_page_only', // Enforce 1st page header rule
  });
  const [setAsDefault, setSetAsDefault] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const initialConfigRef = useRef<PageSetupConfig>(currentConfig);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setDraft({
        ...currentConfig,
        headerRepeat: 'first_page_only',
      });
      initialConfigRef.current = currentConfig;
      setSetAsDefault(false);
    }
  }, [isOpen, currentConfig]);

  const updateDraft = useCallback(
    (newDraft: PageSetupConfig) => {
      const standardizedDraft: PageSetupConfig = {
        ...newDraft,
        headerRepeat: 'first_page_only',
      };
      setDraft(standardizedDraft);
      onLiveChange?.(standardizedDraft);
    },
    [onLiveChange]
  );

  const handleCancel = useCallback(() => {
    onLiveChange?.(initialConfigRef.current);
    onClose();
  }, [onLiveChange, onClose]);

  // Keyboard shortcut listener (Escape to cancel)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        handleCancel();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, handleCancel]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleOrientationChange = (orientation: PageOrientation) => {
    updateDraft({ ...draft, orientation });
  };

  const handleLayoutModeChange = (layoutMode: LayoutMode) => {
    updateDraft({ ...draft, layoutMode });
  };

  const handlePaperSizeChange = (paperSize: PaperSizeKey) => {
    updateDraft({ ...draft, paperSize });
  };

  const handlePageColorChange = (pageColor: string) => {
    updateDraft({ ...draft, pageColor });
  };

  const handleMarginPresetChange = (preset: MarginPresetKey) => {
    updateDraft({
      ...draft,
      marginPreset: preset,
      margins: { ...MARGIN_PRESETS[preset].margins },
    });
  };

  const handleCustomMarginChange = (side: keyof PageSetupConfig['margins'], val: number) => {
    updateDraft({
      ...draft,
      marginPreset: 'custom',
      margins: {
        ...draft.margins,
        [side]: Math.max(0, Number(val) || 0),
      },
    });
  };

  const handleResetToInitial = () => {
    updateDraft(initialConfigRef.current);
    setSetAsDefault(false);
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    onApply(draft, setAsDefault);
    onClose();
  };

  const selectedPaper = PAPER_SIZES[draft.paperSize] || PAPER_SIZES.folio;
  const isLandscape = draft.orientation === 'landscape';
  const liveWidth = isLandscape ? selectedPaper.height : selectedPaper.width;
  const liveHeight = isLandscape ? selectedPaper.width : selectedPaper.height;

  return (
    <div
      style={overlayStyle}
      onClick={handleCancel}
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="page-setup-modal-title"
    >
      <div style={modalContainerStyle} onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        {/* Header */}
        <div style={modalHeaderStyle}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
            <div style={iconBadgeStyle}>
              <SlidersHorizontal size={18} color="#0284c7" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 id="page-setup-modal-title" style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '1rem', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a' }}>
                Page Setup
              </h2>
              <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '0.74rem', color: '#64748b' }}>
                Configure document layout for {reportTitle}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={handleCancel}
            style={closeButtonStyle}
            aria-label="Close page setup modal"
            type="button"
          >
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Content Body */}
        <div style={modalBodyStyle}>
          {/* 1. Layout Mode (Pages vs Pageless) */}
          <div style={sectionGroupStyle}>
            <label style={sectionLabelStyle}>Layout Format</label>
            <div style={buttonToggleGridStyle}>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleLayoutModeChange('pages')}
                style={{
                  ...toggleCardStyle,
                  borderColor: draft.layoutMode === 'pages' ? '#0284c7' : '#cbd5e1',
                  backgroundColor: draft.layoutMode === 'pages' ? '#f0f9ff' : '#ffffff',
                }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                  <FileText size={16} color={draft.layoutMode === 'pages' ? '#0284c7' : '#64748b'} />
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: '0.82rem', color: '#0f172a' }}>Pages</span>
                </div>
                <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 0', fontSize: '0.68rem', color: '#64748b' }}>
                  Divided sheets with Page 1 header, footers &amp; paper bounds.
                </p>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleLayoutModeChange('pageless')}
                style={{
                  ...toggleCardStyle,
                  borderColor: draft.layoutMode === 'pageless' ? '#0284c7' : '#cbd5e1',
                  backgroundColor: draft.layoutMode === 'pageless' ? '#f0f9ff' : '#ffffff',
                }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                  <Maximize2 size={16} color={draft.layoutMode === 'pageless' ? '#0284c7' : '#64748b'} />
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: '0.82rem', color: '#0f172a' }}>Pageless</span>
                </div>
                <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 0', fontSize: '0.68rem', color: '#64748b' }}>
                  Continuous fluid layout with header at the top.
                </p>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 2. Orientation */}
          <div style={sectionGroupStyle}>
            <label style={sectionLabelStyle}>Orientation</label>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12 }}>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={draft.layoutMode === 'pageless'}
                onClick={() => handleOrientationChange('landscape')}
                style={{
                  ...optionCardStyle,
                  opacity: draft.layoutMode === 'pageless' ? 0.5 : 1,
                  borderColor: draft.orientation === 'landscape' ? '#0284c7' : '#cbd5e1',
                  backgroundColor: draft.orientation === 'landscape' ? '#f0f9ff' : '#ffffff',
                }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                  <RotateCw size={15} color={draft.orientation === 'landscape' ? '#0284c7' : '#64748b'} />
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: '0.8rem', color: '#0f172a' }}>Landscape</span>
                </div>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '0.68rem', color: '#64748b' }}>Official DepEd SF1 / SF2 / SF4</span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={draft.layoutMode === 'pageless'}
                onClick={() => handleOrientationChange('portrait')}
                style={{
                  ...optionCardStyle,
                  opacity: draft.layoutMode === 'pageless' ? 0.5 : 1,
                  borderColor: draft.orientation === 'portrait' ? '#0284c7' : '#cbd5e1',
                  backgroundColor: draft.orientation === 'portrait' ? '#f0f9ff' : '#ffffff',
                }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                  <FileText size={15} color={draft.orientation === 'portrait' ? '#0284c7' : '#64748b'} />
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: '0.8rem', color: '#0f172a' }}>Portrait</span>
                </div>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '0.68rem', color: '#64748b' }}>Standard upright format</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 3. Paper Size Selection with Dimension Badge */}
          <div style={sectionGroupStyle}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 6 }}>
              <label style={{ ...sectionLabelStyle, marginBottom: 0 }}>Paper Size</label>
              {draft.layoutMode !== 'pageless' && (
                <span style={dimensionBadgeStyle}>
                  {liveWidth.toFixed(2)}&quot; × {liveHeight.toFixed(2)}&quot; ({isLandscape ? 'Landscape' : 'Portrait'})
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <select
              disabled={draft.layoutMode === 'pageless'}
              value={draft.paperSize}
              onChange={(e) => handlePaperSizeChange(e.target.value as PaperSizeKey)}
              style={{
                ...selectDropdownStyle,
                opacity: draft.layoutMode === 'pageless' ? 0.5 : 1,
                cursor: draft.layoutMode === 'pageless' ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
              }}
            >
              {Object.entries(PAPER_SIZES).map(([key, item]) => (
                <option key={key} value={key}>
                  {item.name} — {item.description} ({item.width}&quot; × {item.height}&quot;)
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* 4. Page Background Color */}
          <div style={sectionGroupStyle}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, marginBottom: 8 }}>
              <Palette size={15} color="#475569" />
              <label style={{ ...sectionLabelStyle, marginBottom: 0 }}>Page Background Color</label>
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
              {PRESET_COLORS.map((c) => (
                <button
                  key={c.value}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handlePageColorChange(c.value)}
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    gap: 6,
                    padding: '6px 12px',
                    borderRadius: 6,
                    border: draft.pageColor === c.value ? '2px solid #0284c7' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                    backgroundColor: c.value,
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    fontSize: '0.74rem',
                    fontWeight: 600,
                    color: '#0f172a',
                  }}
                >
                  <span
                    style={{
                      width: 12,
                      height: 12,
                      borderRadius: '50%',
                      backgroundColor: c.value,
                      border: '1px solid #94a3b8',
                      display: 'inline-block',
                    }}
                  />
                  {c.label}
                </button>
              ))}

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, marginLeft: 'auto' }}>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '0.72rem', color: '#64748b' }}>Custom:</span>
                <input
                  type="color"
                  value={draft.pageColor}
                  onChange={(e) => handlePageColorChange(e.target.value)}
                  style={colorPickerInputStyle}
                  title="Choose custom background color"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 5. Margins Configuration */}
          <div style={sectionGroupStyle}>
            <label style={sectionLabelStyle}>Margins</label>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, marginBottom: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
              {(Object.keys(MARGIN_PRESETS) as MarginPresetKey[]).map((presetKey) => (
                <button
                  key={presetKey}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleMarginPresetChange(presetKey)}
                  style={{
                    padding: '5px 12px',
                    borderRadius: 6,
                    fontSize: '0.75rem',
                    fontWeight: 700,
                    border: draft.marginPreset === presetKey ? '1px solid #0284c7' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                    backgroundColor: draft.marginPreset === presetKey ? '#0284c7' : '#ffffff',
                    color: draft.marginPreset === presetKey ? '#ffffff' : '#475569',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                  }}
                >
                  {MARGIN_PRESETS[presetKey].label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div style={marginInputsGridStyle}>
              <div>
                <label style={miniLabelStyle}>Top (in)</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  step="0.05"
                  min="0"
                  max="3"
                  value={draft.margins.top}
                  onChange={(e) => handleCustomMarginChange('top', parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                  style={numericInputStyle}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label style={miniLabelStyle}>Bottom (in)</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  step="0.05"
                  min="0"
                  max="3"
                  value={draft.margins.bottom}
                  onChange={(e) => handleCustomMarginChange('bottom', parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                  style={numericInputStyle}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label style={miniLabelStyle}>Left (in)</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  step="0.05"
                  min="0"
                  max="3"
                  value={draft.margins.left}
                  onChange={(e) => handleCustomMarginChange('left', parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                  style={numericInputStyle}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label style={miniLabelStyle}>Right (in)</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  step="0.05"
                  min="0"
                  max="3"
                  value={draft.margins.right}
                  onChange={(e) => handleCustomMarginChange('right', parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                  style={numericInputStyle}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 6. Default Persistence Option */}
          <div style={defaultCheckboxContainerStyle}>
            <label style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, cursor: 'pointer' }}>
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={setAsDefault}
                onChange={(e) => setSetAsDefault(e.target.checked)}
                style={{ width: 16, height: 16, accentColor: '#0284c7', cursor: 'pointer' }}
              />
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                <BookmarkCheck size={16} color="#0284c7" />
                <span style={{ fontSize: '0.8rem', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a' }}>
                  Set as default for all reports
                </span>
              </div>
            </label>
            <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 26px', fontSize: '0.7rem', color: '#64748b' }}>
              Saves these dimensions across SF1, SF2, SF4, and future generated registers.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Modal Actions */}
        <div style={modalFooterStyle}>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleResetToInitial}
            style={btnResetStyle}
            title="Reset to initial configuration"
          >
            <Undo2 size={14} />
            <span>Reset</span>
          </button>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10 }}>
            <button type="button" onClick={handleCancel} style={btnSecondaryStyle}>
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={handleSave} style={btnApplyStyle}>
              <Check size={16} />
              <span>Apply Settings</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default PageSetupModal;

/**
 * AttendSure - First-Time Profile Completion Modal
 * File: web/src/components/modals/CompleteProfileModal.tsx
 */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import apiClient from '../../api/client';
import { useTheme } from '../../context/ThemeContext';
import { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { useAlert } from '../../context/AlertContext';
import { UserCheck, ShieldCheck, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

export interface CompletedProfileUser {
  full_name?: string;
  username?: string;
  photo?: string | null;
  photo_url?: string | null;
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

interface CompleteProfileModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onCompleted: (updatedUser: CompletedProfileUser) => void;
}

export const CompleteProfileModal: React.FC<CompleteProfileModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onCompleted }) => {
  const { tokens, brand, fonts, ui } = useTheme();
  const { showAlert } = useAlert();
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    first_name: '',
    middle_name: '',
    last_name: '',
    suffix: '',
    contact_number: '',
    department: 'Junior High School',
    password: '',
  });

  const cleanPhone = formData.contact_number.trim().replace(/[\s-]/g, '');
  const isPhoneValid = /^(09|\+639)\d{9}$/.test(cleanPhone);
  const isPasswordValid =
    !formData.password ||
    (formData.password.length >= 8 && /[A-Za-z]/.test(formData.password) && /[0-9]/.test(formData.password));
  const isFormValid =
    formData.first_name.trim().length >= 2 &&
    formData.last_name.trim().length >= 2 &&
    isPhoneValid &&
    isPasswordValid;

  const setField = (key: keyof typeof formData, val: string) => setFormData((p) => ({ ...p, [key]: val }));

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!isFormValid) return;
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const payload: Record<string, string> = {
        first_name: formData.first_name.trim(),
        middle_name: formData.middle_name.trim(),
        last_name: formData.last_name.trim(),
        suffix: formData.suffix.trim(),
        contact_number: cleanPhone,
        department: formData.department.trim(),
      };
      if (formData.password.trim().length >= 8) payload.password = formData.password.trim();

      const res = await apiClient.put('/v1/accounts/me/', payload);
      showAlert({ title: 'Profile Completed', message: 'Your faculty record is now active.', type: 'success' });
      onCompleted(res.data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      showAlert({ title: 'Error', message: err.response?.data?.detail || 'Failed to update profile.', type: 'error' });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={() => {}} title="Complete Your Profile Information">
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
        <div style={{ ...ui.subtleBox, backgroundColor: tokens.infoLight, borderColor: brand.secondary, display: 'flex', gap: 10, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <ShieldCheck size={20} color={brand.secondary} />
          <span style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.sm, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>
            Please complete your personal details to activate your DTR and class list access.
          </span>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
          <div>
            <label style={ui.label}>First Name *</label>
            <input required style={ui.input} value={formData.first_name} onChange={(e) => setField('first_name', e.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label style={ui.label}>Last Name *</label>
            <input required style={ui.input} value={formData.last_name} onChange={(e) => setField('last_name', e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1.4fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
          <div>
            <label style={ui.label}>Middle Name</label>
            <input style={ui.input} value={formData.middle_name} onChange={(e) => setField('middle_name', e.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label style={ui.label}>Suffix</label>
            <select style={ui.input} value={formData.suffix} onChange={(e) => setField('suffix', e.target.value)}>
              <option value="">None</option>
              <option value="Jr.">Jr.</option>
              <option value="Sr.">Sr.</option>
              <option value="II">II</option>
              <option value="III">III</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
          <div>
            <label style={ui.label}>Contact Number (11 Digits) *</label>
            <input required maxLength={13} placeholder="09171234567" style={ui.input} value={formData.contact_number} onChange={(e) => setField('contact_number', e.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label style={ui.label}>Department *</label>
            <select required style={ui.input} value={formData.department} onChange={(e) => setField('department', e.target.value)}>
              <option value="Junior High School">Junior High School</option>
              <option value="Senior High School">Senior High School</option>
              <option value="Office of the Principal / Administration">Administration</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label style={ui.label}>New Password (Optional, min 8 chars with letters & numbers)</label>
          <input type="password" style={ui.input} value={formData.password} onChange={(e) => setField('password', e.target.value)} />
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', marginTop: 8 }}>
          <Button variant="primary" size="md" type="submit" disabled={saving || !isFormValid}>
            {saving ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <UserCheck size={16} />}
            <span>{saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Complete & Activate Profile'}</span>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default CompleteProfileModal;

# FILE: web\src\components\modals\ProfileModal.tsx
================================================================================
/**
 * AttendSure - User Profile View & Edit Modal
 * File: web/src/components/modals/ProfileModal.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import apiClient from '../../api/client';
import { useTheme } from '../../context/ThemeContext';
import { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { useAlert } from '../../context/AlertContext';
import { Camera, X, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

export interface ProfileUpdatePayload {
  full_name?: string;
  photo?: string | null;
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

interface ProfileModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onProfileUpdated?: (updated: ProfileUpdatePayload) => void;
}

export const ProfileModal: React.FC<ProfileModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, onProfileUpdated }) => {
  const { tokens, brand, ui } = useTheme();
  const { showAlert } = useAlert();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [photoPreview, setPhotoPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    first_name: '', middle_name: '', last_name: '', suffix: '',
    email: '', contact_number: '', position: '', department: '', password: '', photo: null as string | null,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    setLoading(true);
    apiClient.get('/v1/accounts/me/')
      .then((res) => {
        const u = res.data;
        setFormData({
          first_name: u.first_name || '', middle_name: u.middle_name || '', last_name: u.last_name || '',
          suffix: u.suffix || '', email: u.email || '', contact_number: u.contact_number || '',
          position: u.position || '', department: u.department || '', password: '', photo: null,
        });
        setPhotoPreview(u.photo_url || u.photo || null);
      })
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [isOpen]);

  const handlePhotoSelect = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    if (!['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'].includes(file.type) || file.size > 2.5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      showAlert({ title: 'Invalid Image', message: 'Use JPG, PNG, or WebP under 2.5 MB.', type: 'error' });
      return;
    }
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onloadend = () => {
      const b64 = reader.result as string;
      setPhotoPreview(b64);
      setFormData((p) => ({ ...p, photo: b64 }));
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    const payload: Record<string, any> = {
      first_name: formData.first_name.trim(), middle_name: formData.middle_name.trim(),
      last_name: formData.last_name.trim(), suffix: formData.suffix.trim(),
      email: formData.email.trim(), contact_number: formData.contact_number.trim(),
    };
    if (formData.password.trim().length >= 8) payload.password = formData.password.trim();
    if (formData.photo !== null) payload.photo = formData.photo === '' ? null : formData.photo;

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.put('/v1/accounts/me/', payload);
      showAlert({ title: 'Saved', message: 'Profile updated.', type: 'success' });
      onProfileUpdated?.(res.data);
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      showAlert({ title: 'Error', message: err.response?.data?.detail || 'Update failed.', type: 'error' });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const setField = (k: keyof typeof formData, v: string) => setFormData((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: v }));

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose} title="My User Profile">
      {loading ? (
        <div style={{ padding: 24, textAlign: 'center', color: tokens.textMuted }}>Loading profile...</div>
      ) : (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
          <div style={{ ...ui.subtleBox, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 14 }}>
            <div style={{ width: 52, height: 52, borderRadius: '50%', overflow: 'hidden', backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurface, border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderSubtle}`, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
              {photoPreview ? <img src={photoPreview} alt="Profile" style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }} /> : <span style={{ fontSize: '0.7rem', color: tokens.textMuted }}>No Pic</span>}
            </div>
            <div>
              <label style={ui.btnPrimary}>
                <Camera size={12} /> Change Photo
                <input type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={handlePhotoSelect} style={{ display: 'none' }} />
              </label>
              {photoPreview && (
                <button type="button" onClick={() => { setPhotoPreview(null); setField('photo', ''); }} style={{ marginLeft: 8, color: brand.danger, background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: '0.74rem' }}>
                  <X size={12} /> Remove
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
            <div><label style={ui.label}>First Name</label><input required style={ui.input} value={formData.first_name} onChange={(e) => setField('first_name', e.target.value)} /></div>
            <div><label style={ui.label}>Last Name</label><input required style={ui.input} value={formData.last_name} onChange={(e) => setField('last_name', e.target.value)} /></div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1.4fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
            <div><label style={ui.label}>Middle Name</label><input style={ui.input} value={formData.middle_name} onChange={(e) => setField('middle_name', e.target.value)} /></div>
            <div><label style={ui.label}>Suffix</label><input style={ui.input} value={formData.suffix} onChange={(e) => setField('suffix', e.target.value)} /></div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
            <div><label style={ui.label}>Email</label><input type="email" style={ui.input} value={formData.email} onChange={(e) => setField('email', e.target.value)} /></div>
            <div><label style={ui.label}>Contact Number</label><input style={ui.input} value={formData.contact_number} onChange={(e) => setField('contact_number', e.target.value)} /></div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label style={ui.label}>New Password (Optional)</label>
            <input type="password" placeholder="Min 8 characters" style={ui.input} value={formData.password} onChange={(e) => setField('password', e.target.value)} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 8, marginTop: 8 }}>
            <Button variant="secondary" size="md" type="button" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button>
            <Button variant="primary" size="md" type="submit" disabled={saving}>
              {saving ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : 'Save Changes'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default ProfileModal;

# FILE: web\src\components\modals\StudentIdPassModal.tsx
================================================================================
/**
 * AttendSure - Digital ID & QR Pass Modal
 * File: web/src/components/modals/StudentIdPassModal.tsx
 */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useTheme } from '../../context/ThemeContext';
import { isSafeImageUrl } from '../../theme/tokens';
import { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { ShieldCheck, Copy, Check, Printer, User as UserIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import type { StudentItem, AssignedClass } from '../tabs/MyAssignedClassesTab';

interface StudentIdPassModalProps {
  student: (Partial<StudentItem> & { name: string; lrn: string; qr_token?: string; photo_url?: string | null }) | null;
  selectedClass?: AssignedClass | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onCopyLrn: (lrn: string) => void;
}

export const StudentIdPassModal: React.FC<StudentIdPassModalProps> = ({ student, selectedClass, onClose, onCopyLrn }) => {
  const { tokens, brand, fonts, ui } = useTheme();
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  if (!student) return null;
  const safePhoto = isSafeImageUrl(student.photo_url) ? student.photo_url : null;
  const identifier = student.lrn || 'ID-00000';

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={Boolean(student)} onClose={onClose} title="Digital QR ID Pass">
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', textAlign: 'center' }}>
        <div style={{ width: 290, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurface, border: `2px solid ${brand.primary}`, borderRadius: 14, overflow: 'hidden', boxShadow: tokens.cardShadow }}>
          <div style={{ backgroundColor: brand.primary, color: '#fff', padding: 12 }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 6, fontSize: fonts.size.xs, fontWeight: 800 }}>
              <ShieldCheck size={14} /> ATTENDSURE OFFICIAL PASS
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: '0.62rem', opacity: 0.9, marginTop: 2 }}>Macasandig National High School</div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 16 }}>
            <div style={{ width: 54, height: 54, borderRadius: '50%', margin: '0 auto 10px', backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle, border: `2px solid ${tokens.borderSubtle}`, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
              {safePhoto ? <img src={safePhoto} alt={student.name} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }} /> : <UserIcon size={26} color={tokens.textMuted} />}
            </div>

            <div style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.md, fontWeight: 800, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>{student.name}</div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.sm, color: tokens.textSecondary, marginTop: 2 }}>{selectedClass?.section_name || 'Authorized Member'}</div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => { onCopyLrn(identifier); setCopied(true); setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000); }}
              style={{ ...ui.btnSecondary, margin: '10px auto', fontFamily: fonts.mono }}
            >
              <span>ID: {identifier}</span>
              {copied ? <Check size={12} color={brand.success} /> : <Copy size={12} />}
            </button>

            <div style={{ margin: '6px auto', padding: 6, backgroundColor: '#ffffff', border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderSubtle}`, borderRadius: 10, width: 124, height: 124 }}>
              <img
                src={`https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=120x120&data=${encodeURIComponent(identifier)}`}
                alt="QR Code"
                style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'contain' }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle, borderTop: `1px solid ${tokens.borderSubtle}`, padding: 8, fontSize: fonts.size.xs, color: tokens.textSecondary }}>
            QR Token: <strong style={{ color: student.qr_token ? brand.success : brand.warning }}>{student.qr_token || 'Active'}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 14 }}>
          <Button variant="secondary" size="md" type="button" onClick={onClose}>Close</Button>
          <Button variant="primary" size="md" type="button" onClick={() => window.print()}>
            <Printer size={14} style={{ marginRight: 6 }} /> Print Pass
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default StudentIdPassModal;

================================================================================
# FILE: web\src\components\reports\ReportNavBar.tsx
