/**
 * AttendSure - Official Reports Navigation & Export Action Bar (Theme Adaptive)
 * File: web/src/components/reports/ReportNavBar.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useTheme } from '../../context/ThemeContext';
import {
  FileText,
  CalendarCheck,
  Download,
  ChevronDown,
  Printer,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  ExternalLink,
  Table as TableIcon,
  FileCode,
  Loader2,
  RefreshCw,
  Search,
  X,
  Maximize2,
  Calendar,
} from 'lucide-react';

export interface ReportTabItem {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon?: React.ComponentType<{ size?: number; className?: string }>;
}

export interface SectionOption {
  id: number | string;
  name: string;
  grade_level?: any;
  academic_year?: string;
  adviser_name?: string;
  display_label?: string;
}

export interface ReportExportHandlers {
  onExportPdf?: () => void | Promise<void>;
  onExportExcel?: () => void | Promise<void>;
  onExportGoogleSheets?: () => void | Promise<void>;
  onExportWord?: () => void | Promise<void>;
  onExportGoogleDocs?: () => void | Promise<void>;
  onExportCsv?: () => void | Promise<void>;
  onExportJson?: () => void | Promise<void>;
}

export type LayoutMode = 'pages' | 'pageless';

export interface ReportNavBarProps {
  reports?: ReportTabItem[];
  activeReportId: string;
  onSelectReport: (reportId: string) => void;
  academicYears?: string[];
  selectedAcademicYear?: string;
  onAcademicYearChange?: (year: string) => void;
  showAcademicYearFilter?: boolean;
  gradeLevels?: string[];
  selectedGradeLevel?: string;
  onGradeLevelChange?: (grade: string) => void;
  showGradeLevelFilter?: boolean;
  sections: SectionOption[];
  selectedSectionId: string | number;
  onSectionChange: (sectionId: string) => void;
  showSectionFilter?: boolean;
  showDateFilter?: boolean;
  selectedDate?: string;
  onDateChange?: (date: string) => void;
  showMonthFilter?: boolean;
  selectedMonth?: string;
  onMonthChange?: (month: string) => void;
  monthsList?: string[];
  showCalendarYearFilter?: boolean;
  selectedCalendarYear?: number;
  onCalendarYearChange?: (year: number) => void;
  searchQuery?: string;
  onSearchChange?: (query: string) => void;
  showSearch?: boolean;
  layoutMode?: LayoutMode;
  onToggleLayoutMode?: (mode: LayoutMode) => void;
  showLayoutModeToggle?: boolean;
  onRefresh?: () => void;
  isRefreshing?: boolean;
  onOpenPageSetup?: () => void;
  exportHandlers?: ReportExportHandlers;
  onPrint?: () => void;
  isExporting?: boolean;
  exportingText?: string;
  disableActions?: boolean;
}

const DEFAULT_REPORTS: ReportTabItem[] = [
  { id: 'sf1', label: 'Form 1 (School Register)', icon: FileText },
  { id: 'sf2', label: 'Form 2 (Daily Attendance)', icon: CalendarCheck },
  { id: 'sf4', label: 'Form 4 (Monthly Movement)', icon: FileSpreadsheet },
];

const DEFAULT_MONTHS = [
  'January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June',
  'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'
];

const getCurrentMonthName = (): string => {
  try {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { month: 'long' }).format(new Date());
  } catch {
    return 'January';
  }
};

const extractGradeLevelName = (val: any): string => {
  if (val === null || val === undefined) return '';
  if (typeof val === 'string') return val.trim();
  if (typeof val === 'number') return `Grade ${val}`;
  if (typeof val === 'object') {
    const name = val.name || val.level || val.grade_name || val.grade || val.id;
    return name !== undefined && name !== null ? String(name).trim() : '';
  }
  return String(val).trim();
};

export const ReportNavBar: React.FC<ReportNavBarProps> = ({
  reports = DEFAULT_REPORTS,
  activeReportId,
  onSelectReport,
  academicYears = [],
  selectedAcademicYear = '',
  onAcademicYearChange,
  showAcademicYearFilter = false,
  gradeLevels = [],
  selectedGradeLevel = 'ALL',
  onGradeLevelChange,
  showGradeLevelFilter = true,
  sections = [],
  selectedSectionId,
  onSectionChange,
  showSectionFilter = true,
  showDateFilter = false,
  selectedDate = '',
  onDateChange,
  showMonthFilter = false,
  selectedMonth = getCurrentMonthName(),
  onMonthChange,
  monthsList = DEFAULT_MONTHS,
  showCalendarYearFilter = false,
  selectedCalendarYear = new Date().getFullYear(),
  onCalendarYearChange,
  searchQuery = '',
  onSearchChange,
  showSearch = true,
  layoutMode = 'pages',
  onToggleLayoutMode,
  showLayoutModeToggle = true,
  onRefresh,
  isRefreshing = false,
  onOpenPageSetup,
  exportHandlers,
  onPrint,
  isExporting = false,
  exportingText = 'Exporting...',
  disableActions = false,
}) => {
  const { tokens, brand } = useTheme();
  const [isExportDropdownOpen, setIsExportDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const userRole = (localStorage.getItem('attendsure_role') || '').toUpperCase();
  const isAdminOrPrincipal = userRole === 'ADMIN' || userRole === 'PRINCIPAL';

  const filteredReports = useMemo(() => {
    return reports.filter((rep) => {
      if (rep.id === 'sf4' && !isAdminOrPrincipal) return false;
      return true;
    });
  }, [reports, isAdminOrPrincipal]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isExportDropdownOpen) return;

    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (dropdownRef.current && !dropdownRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setIsExportDropdownOpen(false);
      }
    };

    const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        setIsExportDropdownOpen(false);
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [isExportDropdownOpen]);

  const filteredSections = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedGradeLevel || selectedGradeLevel === 'ALL') {
      return sections;
    }
    return sections.filter((sec) => {
      const g = extractGradeLevelName(sec.grade_level).toLowerCase();
      const target = selectedGradeLevel.toLowerCase().trim();
      return g === target || g.includes(target);
    });
  }, [sections, selectedGradeLevel]);

  const availableGradeLevels = useMemo(() => {
    if (gradeLevels && gradeLevels.length > 0) {
      return gradeLevels
        .map(extractGradeLevelName)
        .filter(Boolean)
        .sort((a, b) => String(a).localeCompare(String(b), undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' }));
    }
    const extracted = Array.from(
      new Set(
        sections
          .map((s) => extractGradeLevelName(s?.grade_level))
          .filter(Boolean)
      )
    );
    return extracted.sort((a, b) =>
      String(a).localeCompare(String(b), undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' })
    );
  }, [gradeLevels, sections]);

  const handleExportAction = (action?: () => void | Promise<void>) => {
    setIsExportDropdownOpen(false);
    if (action) action();
  };

  return (
    <div className="no-print" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', width: '100%', flexShrink: 0, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurface, boxSizing: 'border-box' }}>
      {/* 1. Form Type Selector Tabs */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, padding: '10px 24px', backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurface, borderBottom: `1px solid ${tokens.borderSubtle}` }}>
        <span style={{ fontSize: '0.74rem', fontWeight: 800, color: tokens.textMuted, letterSpacing: '0.4px', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>
          REPORT FORM:
        </span>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle, padding: '4px', borderRadius: 10, border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderSubtle}` }}>
          {filteredReports.map((rep) => {
            const Icon = rep.icon;
            const isActive = rep.id === activeReportId;
            return (
              <button
                key={rep.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectReport(rep.id)}
                style={{
                  padding: '6px 14px',
                  borderRadius: 8,
                  border: 'none',
                  fontSize: '0.80rem',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  gap: 6,
                  backgroundColor: isActive ? brand.primary : 'transparent',
                  color: isActive ? '#FFFFFF' : tokens.textSecondary,
                  boxShadow: isActive ? tokens.cardShadow : 'none',
                  transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
                }}
              >
                {Icon && <Icon size={15} />}
                <span>{rep.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 2. Filters & Action Buttons */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', padding: '10px 24px', backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurface, borderBottom: `1px solid ${tokens.borderSubtle}`, flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 12 }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          {showAcademicYearFilter && onAcademicYearChange && academicYears.length > 0 && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '0.78rem', fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>School Year:</span>
              <select
                value={selectedAcademicYear}
                onChange={(e) => onAcademicYearChange(e.target.value)}
                style={{ padding: '6px 10px', borderRadius: 6, border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderStrong}`, fontSize: '0.80rem', fontWeight: 600, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle, color: tokens.textPrimary, outline: 'none' }}
              >
                {!selectedAcademicYear && <option value="">-- Select S.Y. --</option>}
                {academicYears.map((sy) => (
                  <option key={sy} value={sy}>{sy}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {showGradeLevelFilter && onGradeLevelChange && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '0.78rem', fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>Grade Level:</span>
              <select
                value={selectedGradeLevel}
                onChange={(e) => onGradeLevelChange(e.target.value)}
                style={{ padding: '6px 10px', borderRadius: 6, border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderStrong}`, fontSize: '0.80rem', fontWeight: 600, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle, color: tokens.textPrimary, outline: 'none' }}
              >
                <option value="ALL">All Grades</option>
                {availableGradeLevels.map((g) => (
                  <option key={g} value={g}>{g}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {showSectionFilter && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '0.78rem', fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>Section:</span>
              <select
                value={String(selectedSectionId ?? '')}
                onChange={(e) => onSectionChange(e.target.value)}
                style={{ padding: '6px 10px', borderRadius: 6, border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderStrong}`, fontSize: '0.80rem', fontWeight: 600, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle, color: tokens.textPrimary, outline: 'none' }}
              >
                {!selectedSectionId && <option value="">-- Choose Section --</option>}
                {filteredSections.length === 0 ? (
                  <option value="" disabled>No sections available</option>
                ) : (
                  filteredSections.map((sec) => {
                    const gradeStr = extractGradeLevelName(sec.grade_level);
                    return (
                      <option key={sec.id} value={String(sec.id)}>
                        {sec.display_label || `${gradeStr ? `${gradeStr} - ` : ''}${sec.name}${sec.adviser_name ? ` (${sec.adviser_name})` : ''}`}
                      </option>
                    );
                  })
                )}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {showDateFilter && onDateChange && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '0.78rem', fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>Date:</span>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, padding: '4px 8px', borderRadius: 6, border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderStrong}`, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle }}>
                <Calendar size={13} color={tokens.textMuted} />
                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={selectedDate}
                  onChange={(e) => onDateChange(e.target.value)}
                  style={{ border: 'none', outline: 'none', background: 'transparent', fontSize: '0.80rem', fontWeight: 600, color: tokens.textPrimary }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {showMonthFilter && onMonthChange && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '0.78rem', fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>Month:</span>
              <select
                value={selectedMonth}
                onChange={(e) => onMonthChange(e.target.value)}
                style={{ padding: '6px 10px', borderRadius: 6, border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderStrong}`, fontSize: '0.80rem', fontWeight: 600, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle, color: tokens.textPrimary, outline: 'none' }}
              >
                {monthsList.map((m) => (
                  <option key={m} value={m}>{m}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {showCalendarYearFilter && onCalendarYearChange && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '0.78rem', fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>Year:</span>
              <input
                type="number"
                value={selectedCalendarYear || new Date().getFullYear()}
                onChange={(e) => onCalendarYearChange(Number(e.target.value) || new Date().getFullYear())}
                style={{ padding: '6px 10px', borderRadius: 6, border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderStrong}`, fontSize: '0.80rem', fontWeight: 600, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle, color: tokens.textPrimary, outline: 'none', width: 80 }}
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {showSearch && onSearchChange && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, padding: '5px 10px', borderRadius: 6, border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderStrong}`, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle, width: 200 }}>
              <Search size={14} color={tokens.textMuted} />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={searchQuery}
                placeholder="Search name or LRN..."
                onChange={(e) => onSearchChange(e.target.value)}
                style={{ border: 'none', outline: 'none', background: 'transparent', fontSize: '0.78rem', width: '100%', color: tokens.textPrimary }}
              />
              {searchQuery && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onSearchChange('')}
                  style={{ border: 'none', background: 'transparent', cursor: 'pointer', padding: 0 }}
                  title="Clear search"
                >
                  <X size={13} color={tokens.textMuted} />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          {onRefresh && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onRefresh}
              disabled={isRefreshing}
              title="Refresh Record from Database"
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: 6,
                padding: '6px 12px',
                backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle,
                border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderStrong}`,
                borderRadius: 6,
                fontSize: '0.80rem',
                fontWeight: 700,
                color: tokens.textPrimary,
                cursor: 'pointer',
              }}
            >
              <RefreshCw size={14} className={isRefreshing ? 'animate-spin' : ''} />
              <span>Refresh</span>
            </button>
          )}

          {showLayoutModeToggle && onToggleLayoutMode && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onToggleLayoutMode(layoutMode === 'pageless' ? 'pages' : 'pageless')}
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: 6,
                padding: '6px 12px',
                backgroundColor: layoutMode === 'pageless' ? tokens.primaryLight : tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle,
                border: `1px solid ${layoutMode === 'pageless' ? brand.primary : tokens.borderStrong}`,
                borderRadius: 6,
                fontSize: '0.80rem',
                fontWeight: 700,
                color: layoutMode === 'pageless' ? brand.primary : tokens.textPrimary,
                cursor: 'pointer',
              }}
            >
              {layoutMode === 'pageless' ? <Maximize2 size={14} /> : <FileText size={14} />}
              <span>{layoutMode === 'pageless' ? 'Pageless' : 'Pages'}</span>
            </button>
          )}

          {onOpenPageSetup && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onOpenPageSetup}
              disabled={disableActions}
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: 6,
                padding: '6px 12px',
                backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle,
                border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderStrong}`,
                borderRadius: 6,
                fontSize: '0.80rem',
                fontWeight: 700,
                color: tokens.textPrimary,
                cursor: 'pointer',
              }}
            >
              <SlidersHorizontal size={14} />
              <span>Page Setup</span>
            </button>
          )}

          {exportHandlers && (
            <div style={{ position: 'relative' }} ref={dropdownRef}>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsExportDropdownOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                disabled={disableActions || isExporting}
                aria-haspopup="true"
                aria-expanded={isExportDropdownOpen}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  gap: 6,
                  padding: '6px 12px',
                  backgroundColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12)',
                  border: '1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.28)',
                  borderRadius: 6,
                  fontSize: '0.80rem',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  color: brand.success,
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                }}
              >
                {isExporting ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" />
                    <span>{exportingText}</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Download size={14} />
                    <span>Download Report</span>
                    <ChevronDown size={13} />
                  </>
                )}
              </button>

              {isExportDropdownOpen && (
                <div style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 'calc(100% + 6px)', right: 0, width: 270, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurface, borderRadius: 10, boxShadow: tokens.cardShadow, border: `1px solid ${tokens.borderSubtle}`, padding: '6px 0', zIndex: 1000, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
                  {exportHandlers.onExportPdf && (
                    <>
                      <div style={{ padding: '6px 14px 2px 14px', fontSize: '0.62rem', fontWeight: 800, color: tokens.textMuted, letterSpacing: '0.5px' }}>FILE DOWNLOADS</div>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleExportAction(exportHandlers.onExportPdf)}
                        style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: '8px 14px', border: 'none', backgroundColor: 'transparent', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: '0.78rem', textAlign: 'left', width: '100%', color: tokens.textPrimary }}
                      >
                        <Download size={15} color={brand.primary} />
                        <div style={{ flex: 1, textAlign: 'left' }}>
                          <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>Adobe PDF (.pdf)</div>
                          <div style={{ fontSize: '0.68rem', color: tokens.textMuted }}>Official Document Format</div>
                        </div>
                      </button>
                    </>
                  )}

                  {(exportHandlers.onExportExcel || exportHandlers.onExportGoogleSheets) && (
                    <>
                      <div style={{ padding: '6px 14px 2px 14px', fontSize: '0.62rem', fontWeight: 800, color: tokens.textMuted, letterSpacing: '0.5px' }}>OFFICIAL SPREADSHEETS</div>
                      {exportHandlers.onExportExcel && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleExportAction(exportHandlers.onExportExcel)}
                          style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: '8px 14px', border: 'none', backgroundColor: 'transparent', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: '0.78rem', textAlign: 'left', width: '100%', color: tokens.textPrimary }}
                        >
                          <FileSpreadsheet size={15} color={brand.success} />
                          <div style={{ flex: 1, textAlign: 'left' }}>
                            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>Microsoft Excel (.xlsx)</div>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: '0.68rem', color: tokens.textMuted }}>Formatted DepEd spreadsheet</div>
                          </div>
                        </button>
                      )}
                      {exportHandlers.onExportGoogleSheets && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleExportAction(exportHandlers.onExportGoogleSheets)}
                          style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: '8px 14px', border: 'none', backgroundColor: 'transparent', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: '0.78rem', textAlign: 'left', width: '100%', color: tokens.textPrimary }}
                        >
                          <ExternalLink size={15} color={brand.success} />
                          <div style={{ flex: 1, textAlign: 'left' }}>
                            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>Google Sheets</div>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: '0.68rem', color: tokens.textMuted }}>Opens in web spreadsheet</div>
                          </div>
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </>
                  )}

                  {(exportHandlers.onExportCsv || exportHandlers.onExportJson) && (
                    <>
                      <div style={{ padding: '6px 14px 2px 14px', fontSize: '0.62rem', fontWeight: 800, color: tokens.textMuted, letterSpacing: '0.5px' }}>DATA EXCHANGE</div>
                      {exportHandlers.onExportCsv && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleExportAction(exportHandlers.onExportCsv)}
                          style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: '8px 14px', border: 'none', backgroundColor: 'transparent', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: '0.78rem', textAlign: 'left', width: '100%', color: tokens.textPrimary }}
                        >
                          <TableIcon size={15} color={brand.warning} />
                          <div style={{ flex: 1, textAlign: 'left' }}>
                            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>LIS CSV Spreadsheet (.csv)</div>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: '0.68rem', color: tokens.textMuted }}>Standard DepEd data exchange</div>
                          </div>
                        </button>
                      )}
                      {exportHandlers.onExportJson && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleExportAction(exportHandlers.onExportJson)}
                          style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: '8px 14px', border: 'none', backgroundColor: 'transparent', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: '0.78rem', textAlign: 'left', width: '100%', color: tokens.textPrimary }}
                        >
                          <FileCode size={15} color={brand.secondary} />
                          <div style={{ flex: 1, textAlign: 'left' }}>
                            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>Raw JSON Data (.json)</div>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: '0.68rem', color: tokens.textMuted }}>Database export &amp; backup</div>
                          </div>
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {onPrint && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onPrint}
              disabled={disableActions}
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: 6,
                padding: '6px 16px',
                backgroundColor: brand.primary,
                border: 'none',
                borderRadius: 6,
                fontSize: '0.80rem',
                fontWeight: 700,
                color: '#FFFFFF',
                cursor: 'pointer',
              }}
            >
              <Printer size={15} />
              <span>Print</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ReportNavBar;

================================================================================
# FILE: web\src\components\scanner\ClassroomScanner.tsx
================================================================================
/**
 * AttendSure - Classroom QR Attendance Scanner (USB Gun, Camera & Offline Sync)
 * File: web/src/components/scanner/ClassroomScanner.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import apiClient from '../../api/client';
import { API_ROUTES, extractApiError } from '../../api/routes';
import { useTheme } from '../../context/ThemeContext';
import { useAlert } from '../../context/AlertContext';
import { isSafeImageUrl } from '../../theme/tokens';
import { ControlSwitch } from '../ui/ControlSwitch';
import {
  Scan,
  Camera,
  HardDrive,
  QrCode,
  Volume2,
  VolumeX,
  ShieldCheck,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Info,
  X,
  CloudUpload,
} from 'lucide-react';

export interface ScannedStudent {
  student_id: number;
  student_name: string;
  lrn: string;
  section: string;
  status: string;
  scanned_at: string;
  photo_url?: string | null;
  attendance_note?: string;
  is_duplicate?: boolean;
}

interface ClassPeriodOption {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  grade_level: string;
  subject_code: string;
  subject_title: string;
  time_display: string;
}

interface OfflineScanEntry {
  qr_token: string;
  period_id: number | null;
  scanned_at: string;
}

export interface ClassroomScannerProps {
  onClose?: () => void;
  isModal?: boolean;
}

const OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY = 'attendsure_offline_qr_queue';

const loadOfflineQueue = (): OfflineScanEntry[] => {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY);
    return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as OfflineScanEntry[]) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
};

const playFeedbackChime = (type: 'SUCCESS' | 'WARNING' | 'ERROR') => {
  try {
    const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
    if (!AudioCtx) return;
    const ctx = new AudioCtx();
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = type === 'SUCCESS' ? 'sine' : 'triangle';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(type === 'SUCCESS' ? 1046 : 250, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.22);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.22);
  } catch {
    // Browser autoplay policy blocked audio
  }
};

export const ClassroomScanner: React.FC<ClassroomScannerProps> = ({ onClose, isModal = false }) => {
  const { tokens, brand, ui } = useTheme();
  const { showAlert } = useAlert();

  const [scannerMode, setScannerMode] = useState<'USB_GUN' | 'CAMERA'>('USB_GUN');
  const [soundEnabled, setSoundEnabled] = useState(true);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [syncingOffline, setSyncingOffline] = useState(false);
  const [inputCode, setInputCode] = useState('');

  const [periods, setPeriods] = useState<ClassPeriodOption[]>([]);
  const [selectedPeriodId, setSelectedPeriodId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [activeStudent, setActiveStudent] = useState<ScannedStudent | null>(null);
  const [sessionList, setSessionList] = useState<ScannedStudent[]>([]);
  const [offlineQueue, setOfflineQueue] = useState<OfflineScanEntry[]>(loadOfflineQueue);
  const [cameraMessage, setCameraMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const keyBuffer = useRef('');
  const lastKeyTime = useRef(0);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);
  const streamRef = useRef<MediaStream | null>(null);
  const frameRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    apiClient
      .get(API_ROUTES.attendance.classes, { params: { summary: 1 } })
      .then((res) => {
        const list: ClassPeriodOption[] = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : [];
        setPeriods(list);
        if (list.length > 0) setSelectedPeriodId((prev) => prev ?? list[0].id);
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);
  
  const syncOfflineScans = useCallback(async () => {
    const queued = loadOfflineQueue();
    if (queued.length === 0) return;
    setSyncingOffline(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.post(API_ROUTES.attendance.offlineSync, { scans: queued });
      localStorage.removeItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY);
      setOfflineQueue([]);
      showAlert({
        title: 'Offline Scans Synced',
        message: `Synced ${res.data?.synced_count ?? 0} scan(s) (${res.data?.duplicate_count ?? 0} duplicate).`,
        type: 'success',
      });
    } catch (err) {
      showAlert({ title: 'Sync Notice', message: extractApiError(err, 'Still offline.'), type: 'warning' });
    } finally {
      setSyncingOffline(false);
    }
  }, [showAlert]);

  const handleProcessScan = useCallback(
    async (rawCode: string) => {
      const clean = rawCode.trim().replace(/[\r\n]/g, '');
      if (!clean) return;

      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const res = await apiClient.post(API_ROUTES.attendance.scan, {
          qr_text: clean,
          period_id: selectedPeriodId || undefined,
        });

        const d = res.data || {};
        const item: ScannedStudent = {
          student_id: d.student_id,
          student_name: d.student_name,
          lrn: d.lrn,
          section: d.section,
          status: d.status || 'PRESENT',
          scanned_at: d.scanned_at,
          photo_url: isSafeImageUrl(d.photo_url) ? d.photo_url : null,
          attendance_note: d.attendance_note,
          is_duplicate: Boolean(d.is_duplicate),
        };

        setActiveStudent(item);
        if (soundEnabled) playFeedbackChime(item.is_duplicate ? 'WARNING' : 'SUCCESS');
        if (!item.is_duplicate) {
          setSessionList((prev) => [item, ...prev.filter((p) => p.lrn !== item.lrn)]);
        }
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        const netErr = err as { response?: unknown };
        if (!netErr?.response) {
          const nextQueue: OfflineScanEntry[] = [
            ...loadOfflineQueue(),
            { qr_token: clean, period_id: selectedPeriodId, scanned_at: new Date().toISOString() },
          ];
          localStorage.setItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify(nextQueue));
          setOfflineQueue(nextQueue);
          if (soundEnabled) playFeedbackChime('WARNING');
          showAlert({
            title: 'Saved to Offline Queue',
            message: `Internet unavailable. Queued "${clean.slice(0, 20)}" for automatic sync.`,
            type: 'info',
          });
        } else {
          if (soundEnabled) playFeedbackChime('ERROR');
          showAlert({ title: 'Scan Rejected', message: extractApiError(err), type: 'warning' });
        }
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
        setInputCode('');
        setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 40);
      }
    },
    [selectedPeriodId, soundEnabled, showAlert]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const tag = (document.activeElement as HTMLElement)?.tagName;
      if (tag === 'INPUT' && document.activeElement !== inputRef.current) return;
      const now = Date.now();
      if (now - lastKeyTime.current > 120) keyBuffer.current = '';
      lastKeyTime.current = now;
      if (e.key === 'Enter' && keyBuffer.current.length >= 4) {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleProcessScan(keyBuffer.current);
        keyBuffer.current = '';
      } else if (e.key.length === 1) {
        keyBuffer.current += e.key;
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [handleProcessScan]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (scannerMode !== 'CAMERA') {
      streamRef.current?.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
      streamRef.current = null;
      if (frameRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(frameRef.current);
      return;
    }

    let active = true;
    navigator.mediaDevices
      ?.getUserMedia({ video: { facingMode: 'environment', width: { ideal: 640 }, height: { ideal: 480 } } })
      .then((stream) => {
        if (!active) {
          stream.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
          return;
        }
        streamRef.current = stream;
        if (videoRef.current) {
          videoRef.current.srcObject = stream;
          videoRef.current.play().catch(() => {});
        }
        if ('BarcodeDetector' in window) {
          const Detector = (window as unknown as { BarcodeDetector: new (opts: { formats: string[] }) => { detect: (el: HTMLVideoElement) => Promise<Array<{ rawValue?: string }>> } }).BarcodeDetector;
          const detector = new Detector({ formats: ['qr_code', 'code_128'] });
          const tick = async () => {
            if (!active) return;
            if (videoRef.current && videoRef.current.readyState >= 2 && !loading) {
              const codes = await detector.detect(videoRef.current).catch(() => []);
              if (codes[0]?.rawValue) {
                await handleProcessScan(codes[0].rawValue);
                await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1300));
              }
            }
            if (active) frameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
          };
          frameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        setCameraMessage('Camera permission denied. Allow camera access in your browser bar or use USB Scanner mode.');
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
      streamRef.current?.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
      streamRef.current = null;
      if (frameRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(frameRef.current);
    };
  }, [scannerMode, loading, handleProcessScan]);

  return (
    <div style={isModal ? { position: 'fixed', inset: 0, zIndex: 9999, backgroundColor: 'rgba(15,23,42,0.65)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', padding: 16 } : { width: '100%' }}>
      <div style={{ ...ui.card, padding: 0, overflow: 'hidden', width: '100%', maxWidth: isModal ? 900 : '100%' }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 12, padding: '12px 18px', borderBottom: `1px solid ${tokens.borderSubtle}` }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <ControlSwitch
              options={[
                { value: 'USB_GUN', label: 'USB QR Scanner', icon: <HardDrive size={14} /> },
                { value: 'CAMERA', label: 'Device Camera', icon: <Camera size={14} /> },
              ]}
              value={scannerMode}
              onChange={setScannerMode}
            />

            {periods.length > 0 && (
              <select
                value={selectedPeriodId || ''}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedPeriodId(e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null)}
                style={{ ...ui.input, width: 'auto', minWidth: 220, padding: '6px 10px' }}
              >
                {periods.map((p) => (
                  <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
                    {p.grade_level} - {p.name} • {p.subject_code || p.subject_title} ({p.time_display || 'Active'})
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            )}
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
            {offlineQueue.length > 0 && (
              <button type="button" onClick={syncOfflineScans} disabled={syncingOffline} style={ui.btnPrimary}>
                <CloudUpload size={14} /> Sync Offline ({offlineQueue.length})
              </button>
            )}

            <button type="button" onClick={() => setSoundEnabled(!soundEnabled)} style={ui.btnSecondary}>
              {soundEnabled ? <Volume2 size={14} color={brand.primary} /> : <VolumeX size={14} />}
              {soundEnabled ? 'Sound On' : 'Muted'}
            </button>

            {onClose && (
              <button type="button" onClick={onClose} style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: tokens.textMuted }}>
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(320px, 1fr))', gap: 16, padding: 18, backgroundColor: tokens.bgCanvas }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 14 }}>
            {scannerMode === 'USB_GUN' ? (
              <div onClick={() => inputRef.current?.focus()} style={{ ...ui.card, textAlign: 'center', padding: 24, cursor: 'text' }}>
                <div style={{ width: 54, height: 54, borderRadius: '50%', backgroundColor: tokens.primaryLight, color: brand.primary, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', margin: '0 auto 10px' }}>
                  <Scan size={26} />
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '1rem', fontWeight: 800, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>USB QR Scanner Ready</div>
                <p style={{ fontSize: '0.76rem', color: tokens.textSecondary, margin: '6px auto 14px', maxWidth: 400 }}>
                  Scan a signed student QR ID card or type the 12-digit LRN below.
                </p>
                <form
                  onSubmit={(e) => {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    handleProcessScan(inputCode);
                  }}
                  style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}
                >
                  <input
                    ref={inputRef}
                    type="text"
                    autoFocus
                    value={inputCode}
                    onChange={(e) => setInputCode(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Scan QR card or enter LRN..."
                    style={{ ...ui.input, fontFamily: 'monospace' }}
                  />
                  <button type="submit" disabled={loading || !inputCode.trim()} style={ui.btnPrimary}>
                    {loading ? '...' : 'Verify'}
                  </button>
                </form>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div style={ui.card}>
                {cameraMessage ? (
                  <div style={{ ...ui.subtleBox, color: brand.warning }}>{cameraMessage}</div>
                ) : (
                  <div style={{ position: 'relative', height: 260, backgroundColor: '#0f172a', borderRadius: 10, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                    <video ref={videoRef} muted playsInline style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }} />
                    <div style={{ position: 'absolute', width: 165, height: 165, border: `2px solid ${brand.primary}`, borderRadius: 12, pointerEvents: 'none' }} />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {activeStudent ? (
              <div
                key="verified-student-card"
                style={{
                  ...ui.card,
                  borderLeftWidth: 5,
                  borderLeftStyle: 'solid',
                  borderLeftColor: activeStudent.is_duplicate ? brand.warning : brand.success,
                }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
                  <div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '1.02rem', fontWeight: 900, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>{activeStudent.student_name}</div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '0.78rem', color: brand.primary, fontWeight: 700, marginTop: 2 }}>
                      LRN: {activeStudent.lrn} &bull; {activeStudent.section}
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '0.72rem', color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 4, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5 }}>
                      <Clock size={12} /> {activeStudent.scanned_at}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <span style={ui.badge(activeStudent.is_duplicate ? tokens.warningLight : tokens.successLight, activeStudent.is_duplicate ? brand.warning : brand.success)}>
                    {activeStudent.is_duplicate ? 'DUPLICATE' : activeStudent.status}
                  </span>
                </div>
                {activeStudent.attendance_note && (
                  <div style={{ marginTop: 10, paddingTop: 8, borderTopWidth: 1, borderTopStyle: 'dashed', borderTopColor: tokens.borderSubtle, fontSize: '0.74rem', fontWeight: 700, color: activeStudent.attendance_note.includes('CUTTING') ? brand.danger : brand.success, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                    <ShieldCheck size={14} /> {activeStudent.attendance_note}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div
                key="waiting-scan-card"
                style={{
                  ...ui.card,
                  borderStyle: 'dashed',
                  borderColor: tokens.borderStrong,
                  textAlign: 'center',
                  padding: 24,
                }}
              >
                <QrCode size={30} color={tokens.textMuted} style={{ margin: '0 auto 6px', display: 'block' }} />
                <div style={{ fontSize: '0.80rem', fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textSecondary }}>Waiting for First QR Scan</div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div style={{ ...ui.card, padding: 0, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
            <div style={{ padding: '12px 16px', borderBottom: `1px solid ${tokens.borderSubtle}`, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <strong style={{ fontSize: '0.82rem', color: tokens.textPrimary }}>SESSION SCANS ({sessionList.length})</strong>
              {sessionList.length > 0 && (
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setSessionList([])} style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', color: brand.danger, fontSize: '0.74rem', fontWeight: 700, cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                  Clear
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
            <div style={{ height: 340, overflowY: 'auto', padding: 12, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 8 }}>
              {sessionList.length === 0 ? (
                <div style={{ margin: 'auto', textAlign: 'center', color: tokens.textMuted, fontSize: '0.78rem' }}>
                  <Info size={22} style={{ margin: '0 auto 6px', display: 'block' }} /> Scanned students appear here live.
                </div>
              ) : (
                sessionList.map((item, idx) => (
                  <div key={`${item.lrn}-${idx}`} style={{ ...ui.subtleBox, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    <div>
                      <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: '0.82rem', color: tokens.textPrimary }}>
                        #{idx + 1} {item.student_name}
                      </div>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: '0.68rem', color: tokens.textMuted }}>
                        LRN: {item.lrn} &bull; {item.section}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
                      <span style={ui.badge(tokens.successLight, brand.success)}>
                        <CheckCircle2 size={11} /> {item.status}
                      </span>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: '0.64rem', color: tokens.textMuted, marginTop: 2 }}>{item.scanned_at}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ClassroomScanner;

# FILE: web\src\components\tabs\FacultyTab.tsx

/**
 * AttendSure - Faculty & Staff Directory (Theme Adaptive)
 * File: web/src/components/tabs/FacultyTab.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import apiClient from '../../api/client';
import { useTheme } from '../../context/ThemeContext';
import { Pagination, ALL_ROWS_SIZE, exportToCsv } from '../common/Pagination';
import { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { useAlert } from '../../context/AlertContext';
import {
  User,
  Plus,
  QrCode,
  Pencil,
  Trash2,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Camera,
  Loader2,
  Download,
  Search,
} from 'lucide-react';

export interface Faculty {

  employee_id: string;
  first_name: string;
  middle_name?: string;
  last_name: string;
  suffix?: string;

  department: string;
  contact_number?: string;

}

  total_pages?: number;
  current_page?: number;
  results: T[];
}

const FACULTY_POSITIONS = [

  {

      'Head Faculty I',
      'Head Faculty II',
      'Head Faculty III',
