/**
 * AttendSure - Students Directory & DepEd SF1 Enrollment (Zero Mock Data & Role-Aware)
 * File: web/src/components/tabs/StudentsTab.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import apiClient from '../../api/client';
import { API_ROUTES, extractApiError } from '../../api/routes';
import { useTheme } from '../../context/ThemeContext';
import { useSchool } from '../../context/SchoolContext';
import { isSafeImageUrl, sanitizeText } from '../../theme/tokens';
import { StatsCard } from '../common/StatsCard';
import { ProfileAvatar } from '../common/ProfileAvatar';
import { Pagination, exportToCsv, ALL_ROWS_SIZE } from '../common/Pagination';
import { ControlSwitch } from '../ui/ControlSwitch';
import { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { useAlert } from '../../context/AlertContext';
import {
  User,
  Pencil,
  Trash2,
  Loader2,
  CreditCard,
  QrCode,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Phone,
  Printer,
  Camera,
  X,
  AlertCircle,
  Download,
  Plus,
  BookOpen,
  Search,
  Home,
  Users2,
  GraduationCap,
  ShieldCheck,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Student {
  id: number;
  lrn: string;
  first_name: string;
  middle_name?: string;
  last_name: string;
  suffix?: string;
  full_name: string;
  sex: string;
  birthdate?: string;
  mother_tongue?: string;
  ethnic_group?: string;
  religion?: string;
  house_street_sitio?: string;
  barangay?: string;
  municipality_city?: string;
  province?: string;
  father_name?: string;
  mother_maiden_name?: string;
  guardian_name?: string;
  guardian_relationship?: string;
  parent_contact: string;
  qr_token?: string;
  current_section?: string;
  section_id?: number;
  grade_level_name?: string;
  photo?: string | null;
  photo_url?: string | null;
  is_active: boolean;
}

interface GradeLevelItem {
  id: number;
  code: string;
  name: string;
}

interface SectionItem {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  code?: string;
  grade_level?: number | { id: number; name: string };
  grade_level_name?: string;
}

interface MetricsData {
  total: number;
  active: number;
  paired: number;
  total_sections: number;
}

type ModalFormTab = 'BASIC' | 'ADDRESS' | 'PARENTS' | 'DEPED';

const maskQrToken = (token?: string): string => {
  const clean = String(token || '').trim();
  if (!clean) return '';
  return clean.length <= 4 ? 'Linked' : `••••${clean.slice(-4)}`;
};

export const StudentsTab: React.FC = () => {
  const { tokens, brand, fonts, ui } = useTheme();
  const { school } = useSchool();
  const { showAlert, showConfirm } = useAlert();

  const userRole = useMemo(
    () => (localStorage.getItem('attendsure_role') || 'FACULTY').toUpperCase(),
    []
  );
  const canManageStudents = ['ADMIN', 'SUPERADMIN', 'PRINCIPAL', 'DEPT_HEAD'].includes(userRole);

  const [students, setStudents] = useState<Student[]>([]);
  const [gradeLevels, setGradeLevels] = useState<GradeLevelItem[]>([]);
  const [sections, setSections] = useState<SectionItem[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState<number>(1);
  const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState<number>(25);
  const [totalCount, setTotalCount] = useState<number>(0);

  // Strictly zero mock numbers — hydrated 100% from PostgreSQL
  const [metrics, setMetrics] = useState<MetricsData>({
    total: 0,
    active: 0,
    paired: 0,
    total_sections: 0,
  });

  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');
  const [debouncedSearch, setDebouncedSearch] = useState<string>('');
  const [selectedSectionFilter, setSelectedSectionFilter] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [copiedValue, setCopiedValue] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [badgeStudent, setBadgeStudent] = useState<Student | null>(null);
  const [isExporting, setIsExporting] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [formTab, setFormTab] = useState<ModalFormTab>('BASIC');
  const [editingStudent, setEditingStudent] = useState<Student | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    lrn: '',
    first_name: '',
    middle_name: '',
    last_name: '',
    suffix: '',
    sex: 'Male',
    birthdate: '2012-01-01',
    grade_level_id: '',
    section_id: '',
    qr_token: '',
    photo: null as string | null,
    house_street_sitio: '',
    barangay: '',
    municipality_city: '',
    province: '',
    father_name: '',
    mother_maiden_name: '',
    guardian_name: '',
    guardian_relationship: 'Mother',
    parent_contact: '',
    mother_tongue: '',
    ethnic_group: '',
    religion: '',
  });

  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const isInteracting = useRef<boolean>(false);
  isInteracting.current = isModalOpen || Boolean(badgeStudent);

  // Debounce search input by 300ms
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      setDebouncedSearch(sanitizeText(searchQuery, 60));
      setCurrentPage(1);
    }, 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [searchQuery]);

  // Load static lookups (Grade Levels & Sections) once on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    Promise.all([
      apiClient.get(API_ROUTES.school.gradeLevels, { params: { page_size: 200 } }),
      apiClient.get(API_ROUTES.school.sections, { params: { page_size: 300 } }),
    ])
      .then(([glRes, secRes]) => {
        if (!active) return;
        setGradeLevels(Array.isArray(glRes.data) ? glRes.data : glRes.data?.results || []);
        setSections(Array.isArray(secRes.data) ? secRes.data : secRes.data?.results || []);
      })
      .catch(() => {});
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const loadMetrics = useCallback(async (bypassCache = false) => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get(API_ROUTES.students.metrics, {
        params: bypassCache ? { nocache: 1 } : undefined,
      });
      if (res.data) {
        setMetrics({
          total: Number(res.data.total ?? res.data.total_students ?? 0),
          active: Number(res.data.active ?? res.data.total_students ?? 0),
          paired: Number(res.data.paired ?? 0),
          total_sections: Number(res.data.total_sections ?? 0),
        });
      }
    } catch {
      // Keep existing metrics if offline
    }
  }, []);

  const loadStudents = useCallback(
    async (page: number, size: number, silent = false, bypassCache = false) => {
      abortRef.current?.abort();
      const controller = new AbortController();
      abortRef.current = controller;

      if (!silent) setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      try {
        const isAll = size >= ALL_ROWS_SIZE;
        const params: Record<string, string | number> = {
          page: isAll ? 1 : page,
          page_size: isAll ? 'ALL' : size,
        };
        if (debouncedSearch) params.search = debouncedSearch;
        if (selectedSectionFilter !== 'ALL') params.section_id = selectedSectionFilter;
        if (bypassCache) params.nocache = 1;

        const res = await apiClient.get(API_ROUTES.students.list, {
          params,
          signal: controller.signal,
        });

        if (res.data && 'results' in res.data) {
          setStudents(res.data.results);
          setTotalCount(Number(res.data.count) || 0);
        } else if (Array.isArray(res.data)) {
          setStudents(res.data);
          setTotalCount(res.data.length);
        }
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        const anyErr = err as { name?: string; code?: string };
        if (anyErr?.name === 'CanceledError' || anyErr?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED') return;
        if (!silent) setError(extractApiError(err, 'Could not load student directory.'));
      } finally {
        if (!silent) setLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [debouncedSearch, selectedSectionFilter]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    loadMetrics(false);
  }, [loadMetrics]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadStudents(currentPage, pageSize, false, false);
    return () => {
      abortRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, [currentPage, pageSize, loadStudents]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      if (!isInteracting.current && document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
        loadStudents(currentPage, pageSize, true, true);
        loadMetrics(true);
      }
    }, 20000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [currentPage, pageSize, loadStudents, loadMetrics]);

  const modalAvailableSections = useMemo(() => {
    if (!formData.grade_level_id) return sections;
    return sections.filter((sec) => {
      const glId =
        typeof sec.grade_level === 'object' && sec.grade_level !== null
          ? sec.grade_level.id
          : sec.grade_level;
      return String(glId) === String(formData.grade_level_id);
    });
  }, [sections, formData.grade_level_id]);

  const handleCopy = async (val: string) => {
    if (!val) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(val);
      setCopiedValue(val);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedValue((prev) => (prev === val ? null : prev)), 2000);
    } catch {
      // Ignore clipboard failure
    }
  };

  const handleExportCsv = async () => {
    setIsExporting(true);
    try {
      const params: Record<string, string | number> = { page_size: 'ALL' };
      if (debouncedSearch) params.search = debouncedSearch;
      if (selectedSectionFilter !== 'ALL') params.section_id = selectedSectionFilter;

      const res = await apiClient.get(API_ROUTES.students.list, { params });
      const list: Student[] = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : res.data?.results || [];

      exportToCsv(
        `AttendSure_Students_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`,
        [
          'LRN',
          'Full Name',
          'Sex',
          'Birthdate',
          'Class Section',
          'QR Card Status',
          'Parent Contact',
          'Father Name',
          'Mother Maiden Name',
          'Barangay',
          'Municipality / City',
          'Status',
        ],
        list.map((s) => [
          s.lrn,
          s.full_name || `${s.last_name}, ${s.first_name}`,
          s.sex || '',
          s.birthdate || '',
          s.current_section || 'Unassigned',
          s.qr_token ? 'Linked' : 'Not Linked',
          s.parent_contact || '',
          s.father_name || '',
          s.mother_maiden_name || '',
          s.barangay || '',
          s.municipality_city || '',
          s.is_active ? 'ACTIVE' : 'INACTIVE',
        ])
      );
    } catch (err) {
      showAlert({ title: 'Export Failed', message: extractApiError(err), type: 'error' });
    } finally {
      setIsExporting(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePhotoUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    if (!['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'].includes(file.type)) {
      setFormError('Please upload a JPG, PNG, or WebP photo.');
      return;
    }
    if (file.size > 2.5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setFormError('Photo size must be 2.5 MB or smaller.');
      return;
    }
    setFormError(null);
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onloadend = () => {
      setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, photo: reader.result as string }));
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  const handleOpenCreate = () => {
    setEditingStudent(null);
    setFormError(null);
    setFormTab('BASIC');
    setFormData({
      lrn: '',
      first_name: '',
      middle_name: '',
      last_name: '',
      suffix: '',
      sex: 'Male',
      birthdate: '2012-01-01',
      grade_level_id: gradeLevels[0] ? String(gradeLevels[0].id) : '',
      section_id: '',
      qr_token: '',
      photo: null,
      house_street_sitio: '',
      barangay: '',
      municipality_city: '',
      province: '',
      father_name: '',
      mother_maiden_name: '',
      guardian_name: '',
      guardian_relationship: 'Mother',
      parent_contact: '',
      mother_tongue: '',
      ethnic_group: '',
      religion: '',
    });
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEdit = (st: Student) => {
    setEditingStudent(st);
    setFormError(null);
    setFormTab('BASIC');

    const matchedSec = sections.find(
      (s) => s.id === st.section_id || s.name === st.current_section
    );
    const glVal =
      matchedSec && typeof matchedSec.grade_level === 'object' && matchedSec.grade_level !== null
        ? matchedSec.grade_level.id
        : matchedSec?.grade_level;

    setFormData({
      lrn: st.lrn || '',
      first_name: st.first_name || '',
      middle_name: st.middle_name || '',
      last_name: st.last_name || '',
      suffix: st.suffix || '',
      sex: st.sex === 'Female' || st.sex === 'F' ? 'Female' : 'Male',
      birthdate: st.birthdate || '2012-01-01',
      grade_level_id: glVal ? String(glVal) : gradeLevels[0] ? String(gradeLevels[0].id) : '',
      section_id: st.section_id ? String(st.section_id) : matchedSec ? String(matchedSec.id) : '',
      qr_token: '',
      photo: isSafeImageUrl(st.photo_url || st.photo) ? (st.photo_url || st.photo || null) : null,
      house_street_sitio: st.house_street_sitio || '',
      barangay: st.barangay || '',
      municipality_city: st.municipality_city || '',
      province: st.province || '',
      father_name: st.father_name || '',
      mother_maiden_name: st.mother_maiden_name || '',
      guardian_name: st.guardian_name || '',
      guardian_relationship: st.guardian_relationship || 'Mother',
      parent_contact: st.parent_contact || '',
      mother_tongue: st.mother_tongue || '',
      ethnic_group: st.ethnic_group || '',
      religion: st.religion || '',
    });
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);

    if (formData.lrn.trim().length !== 12) {
      setFormError('LRN must be 12 digits.');
      setFormTab('BASIC');
      return;
    }
    if (!formData.section_id) {
      setFormError('Please select a class section.');
      setFormTab('BASIC');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
        lrn: formData.lrn.trim(),
        first_name: sanitizeText(formData.first_name, 100),
        middle_name: sanitizeText(formData.middle_name, 100),
        last_name: sanitizeText(formData.last_name, 100),
        suffix: sanitizeText(formData.suffix, 20),
        sex: formData.sex,
        birthdate: formData.birthdate,
        mother_tongue: sanitizeText(formData.mother_tongue, 80),
        ethnic_group: sanitizeText(formData.ethnic_group, 80),
        religion: sanitizeText(formData.religion, 80),
        house_street_sitio: sanitizeText(formData.house_street_sitio, 150),
        barangay: sanitizeText(formData.barangay, 100),
        municipality_city: sanitizeText(formData.municipality_city, 100),
        province: sanitizeText(formData.province, 100),
        father_name: sanitizeText(formData.father_name, 150),
        mother_maiden_name: sanitizeText(formData.mother_maiden_name, 150),
        guardian_name: sanitizeText(formData.guardian_name, 150),
        guardian_relationship: sanitizeText(formData.guardian_relationship, 60),
        parent_contact: formData.parent_contact.trim().replace(/[\s-]/g, ''),
        section_id: Number(formData.section_id),
      };

      if (formData.qr_token.trim()) {
        payload.qr_token = formData.qr_token.trim().toUpperCase();
      }
      if (formData.photo && formData.photo.startsWith('data:image/')) {
        payload.photo = formData.photo;
      }

      if (editingStudent) {
        await apiClient.patch(`${API_ROUTES.students.list}${editingStudent.id}/`, payload);
        showAlert({ title: 'Student Updated', message: 'Student profile saved.', type: 'success' });
      } else {
        await apiClient.post(API_ROUTES.students.list, payload);
        showAlert({ title: 'Student Enrolled', message: 'New student enrolled successfully.', type: 'success' });
      }

      setIsModalOpen(false);
      loadMetrics(true);
      loadStudents(currentPage, pageSize, false, true);
    } catch (err) {
      setFormError(extractApiError(err, 'Failed to save student record.'));
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = (st: Student) => {
    showConfirm({
      title: 'Delete Student Record',
      message: `Permanently delete ${st.full_name} (LRN: ${st.lrn})?`,
      confirmLabel: 'Delete Student',
      isDestructive: true,
      onConfirm: async () => {
        await apiClient.delete(`${API_ROUTES.students.list}${st.id}/`);
        loadMetrics(true);
        loadStudents(currentPage, pageSize, true, true);
      },
    });
  };

  const totalPages = pageSize >= ALL_ROWS_SIZE ? 1 : Math.max(1, Math.ceil(totalCount / pageSize));
  const activeSchoolName = school.school_name || 'Official Student Pass';

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16, padding: 'clamp(12px, 2vw, 20px)' }}>
      {/* 1. Real Database KPI Cards */}
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(210px, 1fr))', gap: 12 }}>
        <StatsCard
          label="REGISTERED STUDENTS"
          value={metrics.total.toLocaleString()}
          helpText="Students in your accessible scope"
          badge={<GraduationCap size={18} color={brand.primary} />}
        />
        <StatsCard
          label="ACTIVE CLASS LIST"
          value={metrics.active.toLocaleString()}
          valueColor={brand.success}
          helpText="Cleared for classroom QR scanning"
          badge={<User size={18} color={brand.success} />}
        />
        <StatsCard
          label="QR CARDS LINKED"
          value={`${metrics.paired.toLocaleString()} / ${metrics.active.toLocaleString()}`}
          valueColor={brand.primary}
          helpText="Active signed QR tokens"
          badge={<CreditCard size={18} color={brand.primary} />}
        />
        <StatsCard
          label="CLASS SECTIONS"
          value={metrics.total_sections.toLocaleString()}
          valueColor={brand.secondary}
          helpText="Assigned active sections"
          badge={<BookOpen size={18} color={brand.secondary} />}
        />
      </div>

      {/* 2. Section Filter & Actions Bar */}
      <div
        style={{
          ...ui.card,
          display: 'flex',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          alignItems: 'center',
          flexWrap: 'wrap',
          gap: 12,
          padding: '12px 16px',
        }}
      >
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          <select
            aria-label="Filter by class section"
            value={selectedSectionFilter}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setSelectedSectionFilter(e.target.value);
              setCurrentPage(1);
            }}
            style={{ ...ui.input, width: 'auto', minWidth: 220, fontWeight: 700, cursor: 'pointer' }}
          >
            <option value="ALL">All Class Sections</option>
            {sections.map((sec) => (
              <option key={sec.id} value={sec.id}>
                {sec.grade_level_name ? `${sec.grade_level_name} - ` : ''}
                {sec.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <div style={{ position: 'relative', width: 250 }}>
            <Search
              size={14}
              color={tokens.textMuted}
              style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 10, top: '50%', transform: 'translateY(-50%)' }}
            />
            <input
              type="text"
              maxLength={60}
              placeholder="Search student name or LRN..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              style={{ ...ui.input, paddingLeft: 32 }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleExportCsv}
            disabled={isExporting || totalCount === 0}
            style={ui.btnSecondary}
          >
            {isExporting ? <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" /> : <Download size={14} />}
            <span>Export CSV ({totalCount.toLocaleString()})</span>
          </button>

          {canManageStudents && (
            <button type="button" onClick={handleOpenCreate} style={ui.btnPrimary}>
              <Plus size={15} />
              <span>Add Student</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 3. Students Table */}
      <div style={{ ...ui.card, padding: 0, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
        {error && (
          <div
            style={{
              ...ui.subtleBox,
              margin: 14,
              backgroundColor: tokens.dangerLight,
              borderColor: brand.danger,
              color: brand.danger,
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              gap: 8,
              fontWeight: 600,
            }}
          >
            <AlertCircle size={16} />
            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {loading ? (
          <div style={{ padding: 48, textAlign: 'center', color: tokens.textMuted }}>
            <Loader2 size={26} className="animate-spin" color={brand.primary} style={{ margin: '0 auto 8px', display: 'block' }} />
            <span style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.base, fontWeight: 600 }}>Loading student records...</span>
          </div>
        ) : students.length === 0 ? (
          <div style={{ padding: 48, textAlign: 'center', color: tokens.textMuted }}>
            <User size={36} style={{ margin: '0 auto 8px', display: 'block' }} />
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>No Students Found</div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.sm, marginTop: 2 }}>No student records match your current filter.</div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
            <table style={{ width: '100%', minWidth: 740, borderCollapse: 'collapse', textAlign: 'left' }}>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th style={ui.th}>Student</th>
                  <th style={ui.th}>LRN</th>
                  <th style={ui.th}>Class Section</th>
                  <th style={ui.th}>QR ID Card</th>
                  <th style={ui.th}>Parent Contact</th>
                  <th style={ui.th}>Status</th>
                  <th style={{ ...ui.th, textAlign: 'right' }}>Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {students.map((row) => {
                  const fullName = row.full_name || `${row.first_name} ${row.last_name}`;
                  return (
                    <tr key={row.id}>
                      <td style={ui.td}>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
                          <ProfileAvatar photoUrl={row.photo_url || row.photo} name={fullName} size={36} iconSize={17} />
                          <div>
                            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>{fullName}</div>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.xs, color: tokens.textMuted }}>
                              {row.sex}
                              {row.grade_level_name ? ` • ${row.grade_level_name}` : ''}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      <td style={ui.td}>
                        <div
                          style={{
                            display: 'inline-flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            gap: 6,
                            padding: '3px 8px',
                            backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle,
                            borderWidth: 1,
                            borderStyle: 'solid',
                            borderColor: tokens.borderSubtle,
                            borderRadius: 6,
                            fontFamily: fonts.mono,
                            fontSize: fonts.size.sm,
                            fontWeight: 700,
                          }}
                        >
                          <span>{row.lrn}</span>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleCopy(row.lrn)}
                            title="Copy LRN"
                            style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', padding: 0, display: 'flex' }}
                          >
                            {copiedValue === row.lrn ? (
                              <Check size={12} color={brand.success} />
                            ) : (
                              <Copy size={12} color={tokens.textMuted} />
                            )}
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      <td style={ui.td}>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, fontWeight: 700, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>
                          <BookOpen size={14} color={brand.primary} />
                          <span>{row.current_section || 'Unassigned'}</span>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      <td style={ui.td}>
                        {row.qr_token ? (
                          <span style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5, fontWeight: 700, color: brand.success, fontFamily: fonts.mono, fontSize: fonts.size.sm }}>
                            <CreditCard size={13} /> {maskQrToken(row.qr_token)}
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5, color: tokens.textMuted, fontSize: fonts.size.sm }}>
                            <CreditCard size={13} /> Not Linked
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td style={ui.td}>
                        <span style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5, fontSize: fonts.size.sm, color: tokens.textSecondary }}>
                          <Phone size={13} color={tokens.textMuted} /> {row.parent_contact || '—'}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td style={ui.td}>
                        <span style={ui.badge(row.is_active ? tokens.successLight : tokens.dangerLight, row.is_active ? brand.success : brand.danger)}>
                          {row.is_active ? 'ACTIVE' : 'INACTIVE'}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td style={{ ...ui.td, textAlign: 'right' }}>
                        <div style={{ display: 'inline-flex', gap: 6 }}>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setBadgeStudent(row)}
                            title="View / Print Student QR ID"
                            style={{ ...ui.btnSecondary, padding: '6px 8px' }}
                          >
                            <QrCode size={14} color={brand.primary} />
                          </button>

                          {canManageStudents && (
                            <>
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => handleOpenEdit(row)}
                                title="Edit Student"
                                style={{ ...ui.btnSecondary, padding: '6px 8px' }}
                              >
                                <Pencil size={14} color={brand.primary} />
                              </button>
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => handleDelete(row)}
                                title="Delete Student"
                                style={{ ...ui.btnSecondary, padding: '6px 8px', color: brand.danger }}
                              >
                                <Trash2 size={14} />
                              </button>
                            </>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}

        <Pagination
          currentPage={currentPage}
          totalPages={totalPages}
          totalCount={totalCount}
          pageSize={pageSize}
          onPageChange={setCurrentPage}
          onPageSizeChange={(nextSize) => {
            setPageSize(nextSize);
            setCurrentPage(1);
          }}
          itemLabel="students"
          style={{ backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurface, borderTop: `1px solid ${tokens.borderSubtle}` }}
        />
      </div>

      {/* 4. Enroll / Edit Student Modal (Leadership Only) */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={isModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
        title={editingStudent ? `Edit Student: ${editingStudent.full_name}` : 'Enroll New Student (DepEd SF1)'}
      >
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 14 }}>
          {formError && (
            <div style={{ ...ui.subtleBox, backgroundColor: tokens.dangerLight, borderColor: brand.danger, color: brand.danger, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
              <AlertCircle size={15} /> <span>{formError}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <ControlSwitch<ModalFormTab>
            options={[
              { value: 'BASIC', label: '1. Basic', icon: <User size={13} /> },
              { value: 'ADDRESS', label: '2. Address', icon: <Home size={13} /> },
              { value: 'PARENTS', label: '3. Guardian', icon: <Users2 size={13} /> },
              { value: 'DEPED', label: '4. DepEd', icon: <GraduationCap size={13} /> },
            ]}
            value={formTab}
            onChange={setFormTab}
            size="sm"
          />

          {formTab === 'BASIC' && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
              <div style={{ ...ui.subtleBox, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
                <ProfileAvatar photoUrl={formData.photo} name={formData.first_name || 'Student'} size={50} iconSize={22} />
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, alignItems: 'center' }}>
                  <label style={ui.btnPrimary}>
                    <Camera size={13} /> Upload Photo
                    <input type="file" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp" onChange={handlePhotoUpload} style={{ display: 'none' }} />
                  </label>
                  {formData.photo && (
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => setFormData((p) => ({ ...p, photo: null }))} style={{ ...ui.btnSecondary, color: brand.danger }}>
                      <X size={13} /> Remove
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1.2fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
                <div>
                  <label style={ui.label}>12-Digit LRN *</label>
                  <input required maxLength={12} value={formData.lrn} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, lrn: e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '') })} style={ui.input} placeholder="128936000001" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label style={ui.label}>Birthdate *</label>
                  <input type="date" required value={formData.birthdate} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, birthdate: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
                <div><label style={ui.label}>First Name *</label><input required value={formData.first_name} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, first_name: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
                <div><label style={ui.label}>Last Name *</label><input required value={formData.last_name} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, last_name: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
              </div>

              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 0.8fr 0.9fr', gap: 10 }}>
                <div><label style={ui.label}>Middle Name</label><input value={formData.middle_name} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, middle_name: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
                <div>
                  <label style={ui.label}>Suffix</label>
                  <select value={formData.suffix} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, suffix: e.target.value })} style={ui.input}>
                    <option value="">None</option>
                    <option value="Jr.">Jr.</option>
                    <option value="Sr.">Sr.</option>
                    <option value="II">II</option>
                    <option value="III">III</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label style={ui.label}>Sex *</label>
                  <select value={formData.sex} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, sex: e.target.value })} style={ui.input}>
                    <option value="Male">Male</option>
                    <option value="Female">Female</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1.2fr', gap: 10 }}>
                <div>
                  <label style={ui.label}>Grade Level *</label>
                  <select
                    required
                    value={formData.grade_level_id}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, grade_level_id: e.target.value, section_id: '' })}
                    style={ui.input}
                  >
                    <option value="">-- Select Grade --</option>
                    {gradeLevels.map((gl) => (
                      <option key={gl.id} value={gl.id}>{gl.name}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label style={ui.label}>Class Section *</label>
                  <select required value={formData.section_id} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, section_id: e.target.value })} style={ui.input}>
                    <option value="">-- Select Section --</option>
                    {modalAvailableSections.map((sec) => (
                      <option key={sec.id} value={sec.id}>{sec.name}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {formTab === 'ADDRESS' && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10 }}>
              <div><label style={ui.label}>House # / Street / Sitio</label><input value={formData.house_street_sitio} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, house_street_sitio: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
                <div><label style={ui.label}>Barangay</label><input value={formData.barangay} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, barangay: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
                <div><label style={ui.label}>Municipality / City</label><input value={formData.municipality_city} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, municipality_city: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
              </div>
              <div><label style={ui.label}>Province</label><input value={formData.province} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, province: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
            </div>
          )}

          {formTab === 'PARENTS' && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10 }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
                <div><label style={ui.label}>Father&apos;s Name</label><input value={formData.father_name} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, father_name: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
                <div><label style={ui.label}>Mother&apos;s Maiden Name</label><input value={formData.mother_maiden_name} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, mother_maiden_name: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1.2fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
                <div><label style={ui.label}>Guardian Name</label><input value={formData.guardian_name} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, guardian_name: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
                <div><label style={ui.label}>Relationship</label><input value={formData.guardian_relationship} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, guardian_relationship: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
              </div>
              <div><label style={ui.label}>Parent Mobile Number (11 Digits) *</label><input required value={formData.parent_contact} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, parent_contact: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} placeholder="09171234567" /></div>
            </div>
          )}

          {formTab === 'DEPED' && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10 }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
                <div><label style={ui.label}>Mother Tongue</label><input value={formData.mother_tongue} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, mother_tongue: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
                <div><label style={ui.label}>IP / Ethnic Group</label><input value={formData.ethnic_group} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, ethnic_group: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
              </div>
              <div><label style={ui.label}>Religion</label><input value={formData.religion} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, religion: e.target.value })} style={ui.input} /></div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 8, marginTop: 8 }}>
            <Button variant="secondary" type="button" onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}>Cancel</Button>
            <Button variant="primary" type="submit" loading={saving}>
              {editingStudent ? 'Save Changes' : 'Enroll Student'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </Modal>

      {/* 5. Student QR Pass Modal */}
      {badgeStudent && (
        <Modal isOpen={Boolean(badgeStudent)} onClose={() => setBadgeStudent(null)} title="Student QR ID Card">
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', textAlign: 'center' }}>
            <div style={{ width: 292, backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurface, borderWidth: 2, borderStyle: 'solid', borderColor: brand.primary, borderRadius: 14, overflow: 'hidden', boxShadow: tokens.cardShadow }}>
              <div style={{ backgroundColor: brand.buttonBg, color: '#ffffff', padding: '12px 14px' }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 5, fontSize: fonts.size.xs, fontWeight: 800 }}>
                  <ShieldCheck size={14} /> OFFICIAL STUDENT QR PASS
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '0.64rem', opacity: 0.9, marginTop: 2 }}>{activeSchoolName}</div>
              </div>

              <div style={{ padding: '16px 14px' }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center', marginBottom: 10 }}>
                  <ProfileAvatar photoUrl={badgeStudent.photo_url || badgeStudent.photo} name={badgeStudent.full_name} size={56} iconSize={26} />
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.md, fontWeight: 800, color: tokens.textPrimary }}>{badgeStudent.full_name}</div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.sm, color: tokens.textSecondary, marginTop: 2 }}>{badgeStudent.current_section || 'Enrolled Student'}</div>
                <div style={{ margin: '8px auto', padding: '3px 10px', backgroundColor: tokens.bgSurfaceSubtle, borderRadius: 6, fontFamily: fonts.mono, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: fonts.size.sm, display: 'inline-block' }}>
                  LRN: {badgeStudent.lrn}
                </div>
                <div style={{ width: 122, height: 122, margin: '8px auto', padding: 6, backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderWidth: 1, borderStyle: 'solid', borderColor: tokens.borderSubtle, borderRadius: 10 }}>
                  <img
                    src={`https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=120x120&data=${encodeURIComponent(badgeStudent.lrn)}`}
                    alt="Student QR"
                    referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                    style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'contain' }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 14 }}>
              <Button variant="secondary" type="button" onClick={() => setBadgeStudent(null)}>Close</Button>
              <Button variant="primary" type="button" onClick={() => window.print()} icon={<Printer size={14} />}>Print Pass</Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default StudentsTab;

================================================================================
# FILE: web\src\components\tabs\UsersTab.tsx
================================================================================
/**
 * AttendSure - Users & Security Access Management
 * File: web/src/components/tabs/UsersTab.tsx
 *
 * Architecture & Cybersecurity:
 * 1. Single Point Registration: All staff, faculty, leadership, and parents are enrolled here.
 * 2. Automated Directory Filtering: Users with "Can Teach" enabled automatically appear
 *    in the Faculty tab; non-teaching users show only in the Users tab.
 * 3. Mobile App & Portal Integration: Links mobile phone numbers for parent push alerts and app linking.
 * 4. Password Security: Passwords hashed securely on the server with real-time complexity checks.
 * 5. Simple English Terms: Clean labels with Lucide SVG icons (zero mock data).
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import apiClient from '../../api/client';
import { ModuleTableLayout } from './ModuleTableLayout';
import { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { useAlert } from '../../context/AlertContext';
import {
  User,
  Plus,
  Pencil,
  Trash2,
  Loader2,
  Camera,
  X,
  Shield,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Phone,
  Briefcase,
  Building,
  Smartphone,
  GraduationCap,
} from 'lucide-react';

export interface SystemUser {
  id: number;
  username: string;
  employee_id?: string;
  first_name?: string;
  middle_name?: string;
  last_name?: string;
  suffix?: string;
  full_name?: string;
  email?: string;
  contact_number?: string;
  phone_number?: string;
  department?: string;
  position?: string;
  role?: string;
  current_role?: string;
  is_teaching?: boolean;
  can_teach?: boolean;
  is_profile_completed?: boolean;
  faculty_id?: number | null;
  photo?: string | null;
  photo_url?: string | null;
  is_active: boolean;
}

const RBAC_ROLES = [
  { value: 'FACULTY', label: 'Classroom Faculty' },
  { value: 'FACULTY_ADMIN', label: 'Faculty / Admin (Dual Role)' },
  { value: 'ADMIN', label: 'System Administrator' },
  { value: 'PRINCIPAL', label: 'School Principal' },
  { value: 'DEPT_HEAD', label: 'Department Head' },
  { value: 'PARENT', label: 'Parent / Guardian (Mobile App)' },
];

const DEPARTMENTS_LIST = [
  'Junior High School',
  'Senior High School',
  'English Department',
  'Science Department',
  'Mathematics Department',
  'Filipino Department',
  'Araling Panlipunan Department',
  'MAPEH Department',
  'TLE / TVL Department',
  'Values Education (EsP) Department',
  'Office of the Principal / Administration',
  'Parent / Guardian',
];

const POSITIONS_LIST = [
  'Faculty I',
  'Faculty II',
  'Faculty III',
  'Master Faculty I',
  'Master Faculty II',
  'Head Faculty I',
  'Head Faculty II',
  'Secondary School Principal IV',
  'Assistant School Principal',
  'Administrative Assistant',
  'Parent / Guardian',
];

const INITIAL_FORM = {
  username: '',
  employee_id: '',
  first_name: '',
  middle_name: '',
  last_name: '',
  suffix: '',
  email: '',
  contact_number: '',
  department: 'Junior High School',
  position: 'Faculty I',
  role: 'FACULTY',
  is_teaching: true,
  is_active: true,
  password: '',
  photo: null as string | null,
};

export const UsersTab: React.FC = () => {
  const { showAlert, showConfirm } = useAlert();
  const currentLoggedUsername = (localStorage.getItem('attendsure_username') || '').trim().toLowerCase();

  const [users, setUsers] = useState<SystemUser[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState<string>('');
  const [roleFilter, setRoleFilter] = useState<string>('');
  const [teachingFilter, setTeachingFilter] = useState<string>('');

  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [editingUser, setEditingUser] = useState<SystemUser | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState(INITIAL_FORM);
  const [photoPreview, setPhotoPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [copiedKey, setCopiedKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const activeAbortController = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  const [liveErrors, setLiveErrors] = useState<{
    username?: string;
    email?: string;
    password?: string;
  }>({});
  const [isCheckingUsername, setIsCheckingUsername] = useState(false);

  // Real-time field validation
  useEffect(() => {
    const newErrors: { username?: string; email?: string; password?: string } = {};

    if (formData.username) {
      if (/\s/.test(formData.username)) {
        newErrors.username = 'Username cannot contain spaces.';
      } else if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$/.test(formData.username)) {
        newErrors.username = 'Use only letters, numbers, dots, dashes, or underscores.';
      } else if (formData.username.length < 3) {
        newErrors.username = 'Username must be at least 3 characters.';
      }
    }

    if (formData.email && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(formData.email.trim())) {
      newErrors.email = 'Please enter a valid email address.';
    }

    if (formData.password) {
      if (formData.password.length < 8) {
        newErrors.password = 'Password must be at least 8 characters long.';
      } else if (!/[A-Za-z]/.test(formData.password) || !/[0-9]/.test(formData.password)) {
        newErrors.password = 'Password must include both letters and numbers.';
      }
    }

    setLiveErrors((prev) => ({
      ...newErrors,
      username: newErrors.username || (prev.username?.includes('already') ? prev.username : undefined),
    }));
  }, [formData.username, formData.email, formData.password]);

  // Real-time server check for username availability
  useEffect(() => {
    const cleanUser = formData.username.trim();
    if (!isModalOpen || cleanUser.length < 3 || /\s/.test(cleanUser)) return;

    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        setIsCheckingUsername(true);
        const res = await apiClient.get('/v1/accounts/validate-field/', {
          params: {
            field: 'username',
            value: cleanUser,
            exclude_id: editingUser?.id,
          },
        });
        if (res.data?.is_taken) {
          setLiveErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, username: res.data.message || 'Username already taken.' }));
        } else {
          setLiveErrors((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            username: prev.username?.includes('already') ? undefined : prev.username,
          }));
        }
      } catch {
      } finally {
        setIsCheckingUsername(false);
      }
    }, 400);

    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [formData.username, editingUser, isModalOpen]);

  const hasLiveErrors = Boolean(liveErrors.username || liveErrors.email || liveErrors.password);

  const handleCopy = async (text: string, id: string) => {
    if (!text) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setCopiedKey(id);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedKey(null), 2000);
    } catch {}
  };

  const fetchUsers = useCallback(async (query = '', silent = false) => {
    if (activeAbortController.current) {
      activeAbortController.current.abort();
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    activeAbortController.current = controller;

    try {
      if (!silent) setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      const res = await apiClient.get('/v1/accounts/users/', {
        params: { search: query.trim() || undefined, page_size: 'ALL' },
        signal: controller.signal,
      });

      const raw = res.data;
      const items: SystemUser[] = Array.isArray(raw)
        ? raw
        : raw && 'results' in raw && Array.isArray(raw.results)
        ? raw.results
        : [];
      setUsers(items);
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.name === 'CanceledError' || err.name === 'AbortError') return;
      if (!silent) {
        setError('Unable to load user accounts. Administrator permissions required.');
      }
    } finally {
      if (!silent) setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => fetchUsers(search, false), 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(timeout);
  }, [search, fetchUsers]);

  const handlePhotoSelect = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    if (!['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'].includes(file.type)) {
      showAlert({
        title: 'Unsupported Image',
        message: 'Only JPG, PNG, and WebP images are allowed.',
        type: 'error',
      });
      return;
    }

    if (file.size > 2.5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      showAlert({
        title: 'File Too Large',
        message: 'Profile picture must be 2.5 MB or smaller.',
        type: 'error',
      });
      return;
    }

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onloadend = () => {
      const b64 = reader.result as string;
      setPhotoPreview(b64);
      setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, photo: b64 }));
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  const handleOpenCreate = () => {
    setEditingUser(null);
    setFormData(INITIAL_FORM);
    setLiveErrors({});
    setPhotoPreview(null);
    setShowPassword(false);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEdit = (user: SystemUser) => {
    const rawRole = (user.current_role || user.role || 'FACULTY').toUpperCase();
    const isTeach =
      rawRole === 'PARENT'
        ? false
        : user.is_teaching !== undefined
        ? Boolean(user.is_teaching)
        : user.can_teach !== undefined
        ? Boolean(user.can_teach)
        : true;

    const effectiveRole = rawRole === 'ADMIN' && isTeach ? 'FACULTY_ADMIN' : rawRole;
    const phoneVal = user.phone_number || user.contact_number || '';

    setEditingUser(user);
    setLiveErrors({});
    setFormData({
      username: user.username || '',
      employee_id: user.employee_id || user.username || '',
      first_name: user.first_name || '',
      middle_name: user.middle_name || '',
      last_name: user.last_name || '',
      suffix: user.suffix || '',
      email: user.email || '',
      contact_number: phoneVal,
      department: user.department || (rawRole === 'PARENT' ? 'Parent / Guardian' : 'Junior High School'),
      position: user.position || (rawRole === 'PARENT' ? 'Parent / Guardian' : 'Faculty I'),
      role: effectiveRole,
      is_teaching: isTeach,
      is_active: user.is_active ?? true,
      password: '',
      photo: null,
    });
    setPhotoPreview(user.photo_url || user.photo || null);
    setShowPassword(false);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (hasLiveErrors) {
      showAlert({
        title: 'Form Notice',
        message: 'Please resolve the highlighted field errors before saving.',
        type: 'error',
      });
      return;
    }

    const cleanUsername = formData.username.trim().replace(/\s+/g, '');
    if (!cleanUsername) {
      showAlert({
        title: 'Username Required',
        message: 'Username cannot be blank or contain spaces.',
        type: 'error',
      });
      return;
    }

    if (!editingUser && formData.password.trim().length < 8) {
      showAlert({
        title: 'Password Required',
        message: 'Initial password must be at least 8 characters long.',
        type: 'error',
      });
      return;
    }

    const isParentRole = formData.role === 'PARENT';
    const cleanPhone = formData.contact_number.trim();

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const payload: Record<string, any> = {
        username: cleanUsername,
        employee_id: (formData.employee_id.trim() || cleanUsername).toUpperCase(),
        first_name: formData.first_name.trim(),
        middle_name: formData.middle_name.trim(),
        last_name: formData.last_name.trim(),
        suffix: formData.suffix.trim(),
        email: formData.email.trim().toLowerCase(),
        contact_number: cleanPhone,
        phone_number: cleanPhone,
        department: isParentRole ? 'Parent / Guardian' : formData.department.trim(),
        position: isParentRole ? 'Parent / Guardian' : formData.position.trim(),
        role: formData.role,
        is_teaching: isParentRole ? false : formData.is_teaching,
        is_active: formData.is_active,
      };

      if (formData.password.trim()) {
        payload.password = formData.password.trim();
      }

      if (formData.photo !== null) {
        payload.photo = formData.photo === '' ? null : formData.photo;
      }

      if (editingUser) {
        await apiClient.put(`/v1/accounts/users/${editingUser.id}/`, payload);
        showAlert({
          title: 'Account Saved',
          message: `Saved updates for @${cleanUsername}. Role and permissions are now synced.`,
          type: 'success',
        });
      } else {
        await apiClient.post('/v1/accounts/users/', payload);
        showAlert({
          title: 'Account Registered',
          message: `@${cleanUsername} is registered. ${
            payload.is_teaching
              ? 'This faculty member now appears in the Faculty tab.'
              : 'Account is active for mobile or web portal login.'
          }`,
          type: 'success',
        });
      }

      setIsModalOpen(false);
      fetchUsers(search, true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      const errMsg =
        err.response?.data?.username?.[0] ||
        err.response?.data?.employee_id?.[0] ||
        err.response?.data?.error ||
        err.response?.data?.detail ||
        'Failed to save user account.';
      showAlert({
        title: 'Save Failed',
        message: errMsg,
        type: 'error',
      });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const promptDelete = (user: SystemUser) => {
    if (user.username.trim().toLowerCase() === currentLoggedUsername) {
      showAlert({
        title: 'Action Denied',
        message: 'You cannot delete your own active administrator account.',
        type: 'error',
      });
      return;
    }

    showConfirm({
      title: 'Delete User Account',
      message: `Permanently delete account "@${user.username}"? Associated faculty and class list assignments will be unlinked.`,
      confirmLabel: 'Delete Account',
      isDestructive: true,
      onConfirm: async () => {
        try {
          await apiClient.delete(`/v1/accounts/users/${user.id}/`);
          setUsers((prev) => prev.filter((u) => u.id !== user.id));
          showAlert({
            title: 'Account Removed',
            message: `User @${user.username} was deleted.`,
            type: 'info',
          });
        } catch {
          showAlert({
            title: 'Delete Error',
            message: 'Unable to delete user account.',
            type: 'error',
          });
        }
      },
    });
  };

  const filteredUsers = useMemo(() => {
    const q = search.toLowerCase();
    return users.filter((u) => {
      const name = `${u.first_name || ''} ${u.last_name || ''}`.toLowerCase();
      const phone = (u.phone_number || u.contact_number || '').toLowerCase();
      const matchesSearch =
        (u.username || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (u.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (u.employee_id || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        phone.includes(q) ||
        name.includes(q);

      const matchesRole = !roleFilter || (u.current_role || u.role || '').toUpperCase() === roleFilter;

      let matchesTeaching = true;
      if (teachingFilter === 'CAN_TEACH') {
        matchesTeaching = Boolean(u.is_teaching ?? u.can_teach);
      } else if (teachingFilter === 'CANT_TEACH') {
        matchesTeaching = !Boolean(u.is_teaching ?? u.can_teach);
      }

      return matchesSearch && matchesRole && matchesTeaching;
    });
  }, [users, search, roleFilter, teachingFilter]);

  const { activeCount, canTeachCount, cantTeachCount } = useMemo(() => {
    let active = 0;
    let teach = 0;
    for (const u of users) {
      if (u.is_active) active += 1;
      if (u.is_teaching ?? u.can_teach) teach += 1;
    }
    return {
      activeCount: active,
      canTeachCount: teach,
      cantTeachCount: users.length - teach,
    };
  }, [users]);

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 14, padding: 'clamp(12px, 2.5vw, 20px)' }}>
      {/* 1. System Account Summary */}
      <div style={statsGrid}>
        <div style={statCard}>
          <div style={statLabel}>TOTAL REGISTERED USERS</div>
          <div style={statValue}>{users.length}</div>
          <div style={statHelp}>Faculty, leaders &amp; parent accounts</div>
        </div>

        <div style={statCard}>
          <div style={statLabel}>CAN TEACH (IN FACULTY TAB)</div>
          <div style={{ ...statValue, color: '#004b87' }}>{canTeachCount}</div>
          <div style={statHelp}>Visible in Faculty tab &amp; class schedules</div>
        </div>

        <div style={statCard}>
          <div style={statLabel}>NON-TEACHING &amp; PARENTS</div>
          <div style={{ ...statValue, color: '#64748b' }}>{cantTeachCount}</div>
          <div style={statHelp}>Parents &amp; non-teaching accounts</div>
        </div>

        <div style={statCard}>
          <div style={statLabel}>ACTIVE ACCOUNTS</div>
          <div style={{ ...statValue, color: '#059669' }}>{activeCount}</div>
          <div style={statHelp}>Authorized for mobile &amp; web login</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 2. Control Bar with Filters & Add Button */}
      <div style={controlBar}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          <select
            value={roleFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setRoleFilter(e.target.value)}
            style={selectPill}
          >
            <option value="">All Roles</option>
            {RBAC_ROLES.map((r) => (
              <option key={r.value} value={r.value}>
                {r.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <select
            value={teachingFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setTeachingFilter(e.target.value)}
            style={selectPill}
          >
            <option value="">All Status</option>
            <option value="CAN_TEACH">Can Teach (In Faculty Tab)</option>
            <option value="CANT_TEACH">Non-Teaching / Parent</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <Button
          variant="primary"
          size="md"
          type="button"
          onClick={handleOpenCreate}
          style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, backgroundColor: '#004b87', borderColor: '#004b87' }}
        >
          <Plus size={15} />
          Register User / Faculty
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* 3. Main Users Table */}
      <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: 12, border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
        <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
          <ModuleTableLayout
            title="Users & Access Control"
            subtitle="All faculty, school leaders, and parents are registered here. Enabling 'Can Teach' automatically displays the member in the Faculty tab."
            searchPlaceholder="Search by username, name, employee ID, phone, or email..."
            searchValue={search}
            onSearchChange={setSearch}
            loading={loading}
            error={error}
            data={filteredUsers}
            keyExtractor={(u) => u.id}
            columns={[
              {
                header: 'User Profile & Identity',
                render: (u) => (
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
                    <div style={avatarBox}>
                      {u.photo_url || u.photo ? (
                        <img src={u.photo_url || u.photo || ''} alt={u.username} style={avatarImg} />
                      ) : (
                        <User size={18} color="#94a3b8" />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', fontSize: '0.86rem' }}>
                        {u.full_name || `${u.first_name || ''} ${u.last_name || ''}`.trim() || u.username}
                      </div>
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, marginTop: 2 }}>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '0.72rem', color: '#64748b', fontFamily: 'monospace' }}>
                          ID: {u.employee_id || u.username}
                        </span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleCopy(u.username, `user-${u.id}`)}
                          title="Copy Username"
                          style={copyMiniBtn}
                        >
                          {copiedKey === `user-${u.id}` ? <Check size={11} color="#059669" /> : <Copy size={11} color="#94a3b8" />}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ),
              },
              {
                header: 'Role & Faculty Visibility',
                render: (u) => {
                  const canTeach = Boolean(u.is_teaching ?? u.can_teach);
                  return (
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
                      <span style={{ ...rolePillStyle, backgroundColor: '#eff6ff', color: '#004b87' }}>
                        <Shield size={11} /> {u.current_role || u.role || 'FACULTY'}
                      </span>
                      <span
                        style={{
                          ...rolePillStyle,
                          backgroundColor: canTeach ? '#ecfdf5' : '#f1f5f9',
                          color: canTeach ? '#047857' : '#64748b',
                          border: `1px solid ${canTeach ? '#a7f3d0' : '#e2e8f0'}`,
                        }}
                      >
                        {canTeach ? <GraduationCap size={11} /> : <User size={11} />}
                        {canTeach ? 'Can Teach (In Faculty Tab)' : 'Non-Teaching / Parent'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  );
                },
              },
              {
                header: 'Department & Position',
                render: (u) => (
                  <div>
                    <div style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: '#1e293b', fontSize: '0.80rem', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
                      <Briefcase size={12} color="#004b87" /> {u.position || 'Faculty I'}
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '0.72rem', color: '#64748b', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, marginTop: 2 }}>
                      <Building size={12} color="#94a3b8" /> {u.department || 'Junior High School'}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ),
              },
              {
                header: 'Contact & Mobile App',
                render: (u) => {
                  const phone = u.phone_number || u.contact_number || '';
                  const isParent = (u.role || '').toUpperCase() === 'PARENT';
                  return (
                    <div>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: '0.74rem', color: '#334155', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
                        <Phone size={11} color="#64748b" /> {phone || '—'}
                      </div>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: '0.70rem', color: '#059669', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, marginTop: 2 }}>
                        <Smartphone size={11} /> {isParent ? 'Parent Mobile App' : 'Mobile QR & Web Portal'}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                },
              },
              {
                header: 'Status',
                render: (u) => (
                  <span
                    style={{
                      padding: '3px 8px',
                      borderRadius: 12,
                      fontSize: '0.68rem',
                      fontWeight: 800,
                      backgroundColor: u.is_active ? '#ecfdf5' : '#fef2f2',
                      color: u.is_active ? '#059669' : '#dc2626',
                      border: `1px solid ${u.is_active ? '#a7f3d0' : '#fecaca'}`,
                    }}
                  >
                    {u.is_active ? 'ACTIVE' : 'SUSPENDED'}
                  </span>
                ),
              },
              {
                header: 'Actions',
                align: 'right',
                render: (u) => {
                  const isSelf = u.username.trim().toLowerCase() === currentLoggedUsername;
                  return (
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, justifyContent: 'flex-end', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleOpenEdit(u)}
                        title="Edit User & Permissions"
                        style={actionBtn}
                      >
                        <Pencil size={13} color="#004b87" />
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={isSelf}
                        onClick={() => promptDelete(u)}
                        title={isSelf ? 'Cannot delete your active account' : 'Delete Account'}
                        style={{
                          ...actionBtn,
                          backgroundColor: '#fef2f2',
                          borderColor: '#fee2e2',
                          opacity: isSelf ? 0.35 : 1,
                          cursor: isSelf ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                        }}
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={13} color="#dc2626" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  );
                },
              },
            ]}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 4. Registration & Edit Modal */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={isModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
        title={editingUser ? `Edit User: @${editingUser.username}` : 'Register User / Faculty'}
      >
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 14 }}>
          {/* Photo Upload */}
          <div style={photoUploadContainer}>
            <div style={photoPreviewBox}>
              {photoPreview ? (
                <img src={photoPreview} alt="Preview" style={photoPreviewImg} />
              ) : (
                <User size={28} color="#94a3b8" />
              )}
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4, flex: 1 }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '0.78rem', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a' }}>
                Profile Picture
              </span>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '0.68rem', color: '#64748b' }}>
                JPEG, PNG, or WebP under 2.5 MB.
              </span>

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, marginTop: 4 }}>
                <input
                  type="file"
                  ref={fileInputRef}
                  accept="image/png, image/jpeg, image/webp"
                  style={{ display: 'none' }}
                  onChange={handlePhotoSelect}
                />
                <label onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} style={uploadBtnLabel}>
                  <Camera size={13} />
                  <span>{photoPreview ? 'Change Photo' : 'Upload Photo'}</span>
                </label>

                {photoPreview && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setPhotoPreview(null);
                      setFormData((p) => ({ ...p, photo: '' }));
                    }}
                    style={removePhotoBtn}
                  >
                    <X size={12} />
                    <span>Remove</span>
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Credentials: Username & Employee ID */}
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(100%, 180px), 1fr))', gap: 10 }}>
            <div>
              <label style={labelStyle}>Username (Login ID) *</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={formData.username}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, username: e.target.value })}
                style={{
                  ...inputStyle,
                  borderColor: liveErrors.username ? '#dc2626' : formData.username.length >= 3 ? '#059669' : '#cbd5e1',
                }}
                placeholder="Enter login username"
              />
              {liveErrors.username && <span style={errorTextStyle}>{liveErrors.username}</span>}
            </div>

            <div>
              <label style={labelStyle}>Employee or Account ID *</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={formData.employee_id}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, employee_id: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })}
                style={inputStyle}
                placeholder="Enter employee ID"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Full Name Fields */}
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(100%, 140px), 1fr))', gap: 10 }}>
            <div>
              <label style={labelStyle}>First Name *</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={formData.first_name}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, first_name: e.target.value })}
                style={inputStyle}
                placeholder="First name"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label style={labelStyle}>Middle Name</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={formData.middle_name}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, middle_name: e.target.value })}
                style={inputStyle}
                placeholder="Optional"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label style={labelStyle}>Last Name *</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={formData.last_name}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, last_name: e.target.value })}
                style={inputStyle}
                placeholder="Last name"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label style={labelStyle}>Suffix</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={formData.suffix}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, suffix: e.target.value })}
                style={inputStyle}
                placeholder="Jr., III..."
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Contact Details */}
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(100%, 180px), 1fr))', gap: 10 }}>
            <div>
              <label style={labelStyle}>Email Address</label>
              <input
                type="email"
                value={formData.email}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, email: e.target.value })}
                style={inputStyle}
                placeholder="Enter email address"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label style={labelStyle}>Mobile Phone Number (For App Alerts &amp; Parent Linking)</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={formData.contact_number}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, contact_number: e.target.value })}
                style={inputStyle}
                placeholder="Enter 11-digit mobile number"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Role & Teaching Clearance */}
          <div>
            <label style={labelStyle}>System Role *</label>
            <select
              value={formData.role}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const newRole = e.target.value;
                const isParent = newRole === 'PARENT';
                const canTeachDefault = newRole === 'FACULTY' || newRole === 'FACULTY_ADMIN';
                setFormData({
                  ...formData,
                  role: newRole,
                  is_teaching: isParent ? false : canTeachDefault ? true : formData.is_teaching,
                  department: isParent ? 'Parent / Guardian' : formData.department,
                  position: isParent ? 'Parent / Guardian' : formData.position,
                });
              }}
              style={inputStyle}
            >
              {RBAC_ROLES.map((r) => (
                <option key={r.value} value={r.value}>
                  {r.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* "Can Teach" Checkbox (Hidden for Parent role) */}
          {formData.role !== 'PARENT' && (
            <label style={teachingCardStyle(formData.is_teaching)}>
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={formData.is_teaching}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, is_teaching: e.target.checked })}
                style={{ width: 18, height: 18, accentColor: '#004b87', cursor: 'pointer' }}
              />
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 2 }}>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '0.80rem', fontWeight: 800, color: formData.is_teaching ? '#004b87' : '#0f172a' }}>
                  Can Teach Classes (Visible in Faculty Tab &amp; Schedules)
                </span>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '0.70rem', color: '#64748b' }}>
                  {formData.is_teaching
                    ? 'Active: This faculty member will appear in the Faculty tab, sections, and class schedules.'
                    : 'Inactive: Non-teaching user. Will appear only in the Users tab.'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </label>
          )}

          {/* Department & Position */}
          {formData.role !== 'PARENT' && (
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(100%, 180px), 1fr))', gap: 10 }}>
              <div>
                <label style={labelStyle}>Department</label>
                <select
                  value={formData.department}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, department: e.target.value })}
                  style={inputStyle}
                >
                  {DEPARTMENTS_LIST.map((d) => (
                    <option key={d} value={d}>
                      {d}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label style={labelStyle}>Official Position / Title</label>
                <select
                  value={formData.position}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, position: e.target.value })}
                  style={inputStyle}
                >
                  {POSITIONS_LIST.map((p) => (
                    <option key={p} value={p}>
                      {p}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Password */}
          <div>
            <label style={labelStyle}>
              {editingUser ? 'Reset Password (Optional, min 8 chars)' : 'Initial Password (Min 8 chars) *'}
            </label>
            <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
              <input
                type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                required={!editingUser}
                minLength={8}
                placeholder={editingUser ? 'Leave blank to keep current password' : 'Minimum 8 characters'}
                value={formData.password}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, password: e.target.value })}
                style={{ ...inputStyle, paddingRight: 36 }}
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                style={eyeToggleBtn}
              >
                {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={15} /> : <Eye size={15} />}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 8, marginTop: 8 }}>
            <Button variant="secondary" size="md" type="button" onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="primary"
              size="md"
              type="submit"
              disabled={saving || hasLiveErrors || isCheckingUsername}
              style={{ backgroundColor: '#004b87', borderColor: '#004b87' }}
            >
              {saving ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={16} /> : null}
              {editingUser ? 'Save Updates' : 'Register User'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
};
